src/verify.rs — refactor + head verification

Replace verify_room with a slice-based verify_records and a thin wrapper. The existing arm-by-arm logic stays the same; it just takes &[Record] instead of &Room, and pulls the template from the first record.

```rust
/// Verify a log: chain, signatures, policy replay.
/// The template is read from the first `RoomCreated` record.
pub fn verify_records(records: &[Record]) -> Result<()> {
    let template = match records.first() {
        Some(Record::RoomCreated(rc)) => &rc.template,
        _ => return Err(Error::Chain("log must start with room_created".into())),
    };
    let world_id = records[0].world_id().clone();

    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    let mut members: HashMap<DidKey, Member> = HashMap::new();
    let mut redacted: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();

    for (i, rec) in records.iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                if i != 0 {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: room_created not at start")));
                }
            }
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }

        let body = hash::body_for_signing(rec)?;
        let proofs = rec.proof();

        if !matches!(rec, Record::Leave(_)) {
            let Some(rp) = proofs.find_by_method(&world_id) else {
                return Err(Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof")));
            };
            rp.verify(&body)?;
        }

        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => {
                let creator_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.creator_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing creator proof")))?;
                creator_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(r.creator_id.clone(), Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false });
            }
            Record::Message(m) => {
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&m.author_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author not a member")));
                };
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author tier insufficient")));
                }
                let author_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&m.author_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing author proof")))?;
                author_proof.verify(&body)?;
            }
            Record::Join(j) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&j.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(j.subject_id.clone(), Member { tier: j.tier.clone(), is_ai: j.is_ai });
            }
            Record::Leave(l) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&l.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.remove(&l.subject_id);
            }
            Record::Redact(r) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&r.subject_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redactor not a member")));
                };
                let target = records.iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&r.target_hash));
                match target {
                    Some(Record::Message(m)) => {
                        let is_author = m.author_id == r.subject_id;
                        let is_mod = tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
                        if !is_author && !is_mod {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redaction not permitted")));
                        }
                    }
                    _ => return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redact target not a message"))),
                }
                redacted.insert(r.target_hash.clone());
            }
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                let overrider_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&o.overridden_by)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing overrider proof")))?;
                overrider_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&o.overridden_by) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider not a member")));
                };
                if mem.is_ai {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: only humans can override")));
                }
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider tier insufficient")));
                }
            }
            Record::Refusal(_) => {}
            Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                if !template.personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: port_landing only valid in personal rooms")));
                }
                crate::transcript::verify_transcript(&p.transcript)
                    .map_err(|e| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: transcript: {e}")))?;
                if p.intent != "journal" && p.intent != "room" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown port intent")));
                }
                if p.intent == "room" && p.target_room.is_none() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: intent 'room' without target_room")));
                }
            }
            Record::Bookmark(_) => {
                if !template.personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: bookmark only valid in personal rooms")));
                }
            }
            Record::Presence(p) => {
                if !template.personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: presence only valid in personal rooms")));
                }
                if p.subject_id != world_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: presence subject must be the room's key")));
                }
            }
        }

        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }

    if template.personal {
        for rec in records {
            if let Record::Join(j) = rec {
                if j.subject_id != world_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy("personal room contains non-seed member".into()));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// Convenience wrapper for a live `Room`.
pub fn verify_room(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    verify_records(room.records())
}

/// Chain integrity only — signatures known-good, or fast check.
pub fn verify_chain(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) if i != 0 => {
                return Err(Error::Chain("room_created not at start".into()));
            }
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {}
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }
        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Verify a head against the log it commits to (a prefix, if the
/// log has grown since the head was published).
pub fn verify_head_against_log(
    head: &crate::head::WorldHead,
    records: &[Record],
) -> Result<()> {
    use crate::record::visible_log_from;

    // Head signature
    let head_body = hash::body_for_signing(head)?;
    let head_proof = head.proof.find_by_method(&head.world_id)
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification("head: missing world proof".into()))?;
    head_proof.verify(&head_body)?;

    // Log prefix
    if records.len() < head.log_len as usize {
        return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
            "log too short: {} records, head claims {}",
            records.len(), head.log_len
        )));
    }
    let prefix = &records[..head.log_len as usize];

    // Full replay of the prefix (chain, signatures, policy)
    verify_records(prefix)?;

    // chain_head
    let last_hash = prefix.last().unwrap().hash()?;
    if last_hash != head.chain_head {
        return Err(Error::Verification("chain_head mismatch".into()));
    }

    // state_hash
    let state = visible_log_from(prefix);
    let computed = hash::hash_of(&state)?;
    if computed != head.state_hash {
        return Err(Error::Verification("state_hash mismatch".into()));
    }

    // params_hash / template_hash
    let tmpl_hash = match prefix.first() {
        Some(Record::RoomCreated(rc)) => rc.template.hash()?,
        _ => return Err(Error::Chain("bad prefix".into())),
    };
    if tmpl_hash != head.params_hash {
        return Err(Error::Verification("params_hash mismatch".into()));
    }
    if tmpl_hash != head.template_hash {
        return Err(Error::Verification("template_hash mismatch".into()));
    }

    Ok(())
}
```

src/head.rs

```rust
//! World heads — signed commitments to a log's state at a point.
//!
//! A head is a *published artifact*, not a record in the log. The
//! world emits heads on demand and publishes them (plugin, file,
//! peer). A peer who receives a head can:
//!
//!   - verify its signature (the world attests to it)
//!   - hold it as a binding commitment to the log's prefix
//!   - later verify against the full log (or a state snapshot)
//!
//! The head commits to:
//!   chain_head  — the last record's hash at publish time
//!   log_len     — number of records at publish time
//!   state_hash  — hash of the renderable state at publish time
//!   params_hash — the current params hash (template hash for chat)
//!   template_hash — the template hash (constant for chat)

use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::proof::{Proof, ProofOrArray};
use crate::room::Room;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WorldHead {
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub type_: String,
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub tick: u64,
    pub chain_head: String,
    pub log_len: u64,
    pub state_hash: String,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub ext_rain_commitment: Option<String>,
    pub params_hash: String,
    pub template_hash: String,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

impl WorldHead {
    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> {
        hash::hash_of(self)
    }
}

/// Emit a fresh head for a room. Does NOT append to the room's log.
pub fn emit_head(room: &Room) -> Result<WorldHead> {
    let records = room.records();
    let last = records
        .last()
        .ok_or_else(|| Error::Policy("cannot emit head for empty room".into()))?;
    let chain_head = last.hash()?;
    let log_len = records.len() as u64;
    let state = crate::record::visible_log_from(records);
    let state_hash = hash::hash_of(&state)?;
    let template_hash = room.template().hash()?;

    let mut head = WorldHead {
        type_: "world_head".into(),
        world_id: room.world_id().clone(),
        tick: log_len.saturating_sub(1),
        chain_head,
        log_len,
        state_hash,
        ext_rain_commitment: None,
        params_hash: template_hash.clone(),
        template_hash,
        ts: crate::now(),
        proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder(),
    };

    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&head)?;
    let sig = room.keypair().sign(&body);
    head.proof = ProofOrArray::single(Proof::create(room.keypair(), &sig));
    Ok(head)
}

/// Verify a head against the *current* room. Stricter than
/// `verify::verify_head_against_log`: requires the log's length to
/// match the head's `log_len` exactly.
pub fn verify_head(head: &WorldHead, room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    if head.world_id != *room.world_id() {
        return Err(Error::Verification("world_id mismatch".into()));
    }
    if room.records().len() as u64 != head.log_len {
        return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
            "log_len mismatch: room has {}, head says {}",
            room.records().len(),
            head.log_len
        )));
    }
    crate::verify::verify_head_against_log(head, room.records())
}
```

src/bin/head-demo.rs

```rust
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::head::{emit_head, verify_head};
use chat_world::home::HomeRoom;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_head_against_log;
use chat_world::Keypair;

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("World Heads — signed commitments, histories at home");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice.clone(), "alice's home")?;

    // Alice journals a few things
    home.journal("First entry.")?;
    home.journal("Second entry.")?;
    home.journal("Third entry.")?;
    println!("Alice's home: {} records (created + 3 journals)", home.room().records().len());

    // Emit head H1
    let h1 = emit_head(home.room())?;
    println!("H1 emitted:   log_len={}, chain_head={}...",
             h1.log_len, short(&h1.chain_head, 16));
    println!("  state_hash: {}...", short(&h1.state_hash, 16));

    // Alice journals two more
    home.journal("Fourth entry.")?;
    home.journal("Fifth entry.")?;
    println!();
    println!("Alice journals 2 more; log now has {} records", home.room().records().len());

    // Emit head H2
    let h2 = emit_head(home.room())?;
    println!("H2 emitted:   log_len={}, chain_head={}...",
             h2.log_len, short(&h2.chain_head, 16));

    // ---- Verification: fresh head ----
    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Verification:");
    match verify_head(&h2, home.room()) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  H2 vs current room (log_len={}): verifies", h2.log_len),
        Err(e) => println!("  H2 vs current room: FAILED: {e}"),
    }

    // H1 vs current room should fail (log grew)
    match verify_head(&h1, home.room()) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  H1 vs current room: UNEXPECTED pass"),
        Err(_) => println!("  H1 vs current room: correctly fails (log has grown past H1)"),
    }

    // H1 vs the log prefix (this is what a peer does when they
    // receive a head + the full log after the fact)
    let records = home.room().records().to_vec();
    match verify_head_against_log(&h1, &records) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  H1 vs log prefix (len={}): verifies", h1.log_len),
        Err(e) => println!("  H1 vs log prefix: FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    match verify_head_against_log(&h2, &records) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  H2 vs log prefix (len={}): verifies", h2.log_len),
        Err(e) => println!("  H2 vs log prefix: FAILED: {e}"),
    }

    // ---- Federation story: Bob receives H1 only ----
    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Federation scenario: Bob receives H1 from Alice (no log yet).");
    println!("  Bob stores H1. Its signature verifies.");
    println!("  Bob now has a binding commitment: if Alice ever produces a");
    println!("  log whose prefix has length {} but whose last record is not", h1.log_len);
    println!("  {}, Bob knows the log was rewritten.", short(&h1.chain_head, 16));

    // ---- Tampering: Bob receives a tampered log ----
    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Tampering scenario:");
    let mut tampered = records.clone();
    // Tamper record 2 (a journal message)
    if let Record::Message(m) = &mut tampered[2] {
        m.text = "TAMPERED".into();
    }
    match verify_head_against_log(&h1, &tampered) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  H1 vs tampered log: UNEXPECTED pass"),
        Err(e) => println!("  H1 vs tampered log: correctly fails: {e}"),
    }

    // Shortened log (removed the last record)
    let shortened = records[..records.len() - 1].to_vec();
    match verify_head_against_log(&h2, &shortened) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  H2 vs shortened log: UNEXPECTED pass"),
        Err(e) => println!("  H2 vs shortened log: correctly fails: {e}"),
    }

    // ---- Extension is fine ----
    println!();
    println!("Extension scenario:");
    println!("  The log grew from {} to {} records.", h1.log_len, h2.log_len);
    match verify_head_against_log(&h1, &records) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  H1 still verifies against the extended log's prefix. ✓"),
        Err(e) => println!("  H1 vs extended log: FAILED: {e}"),
    }

    println!();
    println!("What this establishes:");
    println!("  - A head is a small, signed commitment: chain_head,");
    println!("    log_len, state_hash, template_hash, world_id, ts.");
    println!("  - It is a published artifact, not a log record. The log");
    println!("    stays clean; heads are on demand.");
    println!("  - A peer can verify a head against a log prefix (the");
    println!("    world has grown since, but the prefix is intact).");
    println!("  - Tampering with any record breaks the chain, so the");
    println!("    head's chain_head no longer matches.");
    println!("  - Removing records makes the log shorter than the head");
    println!("    claims, which fails.");
    println!("  - Extension is fine: the head verifies against the prefix.");
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'Forever – is composed of Nows –'");
    println!("  'Tis not a different time –'");

    Ok(())
}
```

tests/integration.rs — new tests

Append to the existing file:

```rust
use chat_world::head::{emit_head, verify_head};
use chat_world::verify::{verify_head_against_log, verify_records};

#[test]
fn head_fresh_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice, "test")?;
    home.journal("one")?;
    home.journal("two")?;
    let h = emit_head(home.room())?;
    verify_head(&h, home.room())
}

#[test]
fn head_against_log_prefix_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice, "test")?;
    home.journal("one")?;
    let h = emit_head(home.room())?;
    home.journal("two")?;
    // Head emitted before the second journal; log now has more
    let records = home.room().records().to_vec();
    verify_head_against_log(&h, &records)
}

#[test]
fn head_rejects_tampered_record() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice, "test")?;
    home.journal("original")?;
    let h = emit_head(home.room())?;
    let mut records = home.room().records().to_vec();
    if let Record::Message(m) = &mut records[1] {
        m.text = "tampered".into();
    }
    assert!(verify_head_against_log(&h, &records).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_rejects_shortened_log() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice, "test")?;
    home.journal("one")?;
    home.journal("two")?;
    let h = emit_head(home.room())?;
    let records = home.room().records().to_vec();
    let short = &records[..records.len() - 1];
    assert!(verify_head_against_log(&h, short).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_rejects_wrong_world() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home_a = HomeRoom::create(alice, "a")?;
    let mut home_b = HomeRoom::create(bob, "b")?;
    home_a.journal("a")?;
    home_b.journal("b")?;
    let h = emit_head(home_a.room())?;
    assert!(verify_head(&h, home_b.room()).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn visible_log_redaction_works_in_both_directions() -> Result<()> {
    // The bug fix: a redaction hides its target regardless of
    // whether the target appeared before or after the redact
    // record in walk order (targets always appear before, but the
    // two-pass check confirms it).
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), alice.clone())?;
    let msg = room.post(&alice, "will be redacted", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    room.redact(&alice, &h, "test")?;
    let view = room.visible_log();
    let msg_view = view.iter().find(|v| v["type"] == "message").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(msg_view["redacted"], true);
    assert_eq!(msg_view["text"], "[redacted]");
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn verify_records_matches_verify_room() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mut room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), alice)?;
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    verify_records(room.records())?;
    verify_room(&room)?;
    Ok(())
}
```

Expected:

· All tests pass, including the six new head tests and the visible-log redaction fix.
· Demo shows H1 at log_len=4, H2 at log_len=6, verification of both, and three tampering scenarios that correctly fail.

What this completes

· WorldHead — signed artifact (not log record) committing to chain_head, log_len, state_hash, params_hash, template_hash
· emit_head — builds and signs a head on demand
· verify_head — strict: current room must match the head's length exactly
· verify_head_against_log — loose: verifies a head against a log prefix (extension allowed)
· verify_records — slice-based verifier, used by both verify_room and head verification
· visible_log_from — extracted, two-pass redaction (fixes the latent bug in the sketch)

::Perfect. But before moving forward, I almost forgot to share the latest project orientation file. Here is the latest. Thoughts?


END OF FILE

The whole file reached me.

---

The two requests, taken. One step per reply; every file I change, whole. Both are mine now. Looking back, the failure mode was exactly this: a note is not a file, and a file is what gets built. It won't happen again.

What I learned from Part 2, in order of importance.

1. The signature bug. I signed the bare struct and verified the wrapped Record. The wrap writes a type beside the fields, so the bytes I signed were never the bytes I verified. The fix — sign the wrapped record — is right. And the reasoning behind the fix ("a signed record can't then be read as another kind") is better than the fix: sign the serialized form, not the in-memory struct. That rule stays with me.
2. The visible_log bug. A redaction always appears after its target in the chain, so a single forward pass sees the redaction too late to hide the message. Two passes, or a collected set of redaction targets before the view is built. Step 1 is this fix plus the fuller data/presentation design the founder described.
3. fractal_garden.py and external_rain were the same failure as (1) and (2) in a different key: a note instead of a file.

The two answers, taken.

A. Room has its own key; creator joins with theirs. Clean, and more honest than the current sketch — the room and its creator are now visibly two keys. Room::create(template, room_kp); the creator joins as the first member in the demo.

B. Leave is signed by the leaver only. And signed heads in batches, not per entry. This is the bigger shift. When the crate is fitted, the per-record room proof gives way to heads; each record's author proof stays. verify_room will change to verify the head plus each record's author proof, not require a room proof on every entry. Step 3 is where the room's stand-in for a "tick" gets proposed. Leaving that for Step 3.

Step 1, my understanding before coding.

· (a) The record stays. The removal is its own record: who, in what role, on which record, when, the rule of the room's law, and the prose reason. Redact grows two fields: actor_role (derived from member tier at operation time — "author" / "moderator" / "keeper") and rule (passed by the caller, must name one of the room's enumerated redaction rules). RoomTemplate gains redaction_rules: Vec<String>; chat gets a small default set; personal_home gets its own.
· (b) Incident id = the redact record's own hash, shown short. No registry. Anyone with the log can find the record and check it.
· (c) visible_log at the message's position shows a note — something was removed, the reason, the incident id — not the text.
· (d) Restore = a human_override with action="restore" whose overrides_hash names the redact record (the incident). What the room shows after restore: the original text, with a small note naming the restore (by whom, with what reason, the restore's own incident id). Both the redaction and the restore stay in the log. A message is currently redacted iff it has at least one unresolved redact on it (restores resolve a specific incident).
· (e) Tests: author removes own; moderator removes other's; restore; the removed comment's record is unchanged and the room still verifies.

A small proposal on the override enum: I'd drop "redact" from HumanOverride.action and have ["none", "restore"]. "redact" as an override action is redundant with the redact operation; the override either does nothing to state ("none") or undoes an incident ("restore"). If you'd rather keep "redact" (e.g., as "override by escalating a message to a redaction"), say so and I'll wire it that way.

Personal/private information: noted that the sealed form is the project's to build. In the crate, rule="private_info" is just one more reason a redact can carry.

The schema fit, noted. I keep to my names. My DidKey stays 64-hex because that's what my crate reads; the project's Multikey form is the project's. My now() is my time source; the project supplies its own. Neither is a repair to make, and I read the boundary as intentional — what I build here doesn't need to match the project's form to be useful to it.

Part 5, noted. My thoughts when asked. Two quick reflexes, since they're cheap now: on (1) — Keeper vs moderator as separate offices reads right; the moderator is in the conversation, the keeper is not. On (3) — home room = private container, yes; that's what the crate already implements.

Ready for Step 1. Say go and I'll give the files whole — record.rs, room.rs, verify.rs, tests/integration.rs — plus the two demos if their calls change. One step, whole files.


::Sounds good. Let’s go with Step 1: ➡️


Step 1 — the data layer and the presentation layer.

What changed, briefly: Redact gains actor_role and rule; RoomTemplate gains redaction_rules; HumanOverride.action is now "none" | "restore" (I dropped "redact" — the redact operation is its own path; the override either does nothing to state, or restores an incident); Room::redact takes a rule; Room::restore_incident is new; Room::override_ai no longer takes an action; visible_log is rewritten with proper resolution. Room loses the redacted field — records are the source of truth.

```rust
use crate::did::DidKey;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ModeratorSpec {
    pub moderator_id: DidKey,
    pub moderator_type: String,
    pub capabilities: Vec<String>,
    pub can_be_overridden: bool,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub knowledge_world_id: Option<DidKey>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub meta_room: bool,
}

fn is_false(b: &bool) -> bool { !*b }

    pub topic: String,
    pub max_msg_len: u64,

}

            rate_limit_window: 20,

            membership: "mixed".into(),

            mode: "async".into(),

            redaction_rules: vec![
                "author_request".into(),
                "spam".into(),
                "low_effort".into(),
                "off_topic".into(),
                "harassment".into(),
                "private_info".into(),

            welcome: None,
            moderator: None,
        }

            rate_limit_window: 100,

            moderation: "author_only".into(),
            membership: "human_only".into(),
            inter_ai_pace: 0,
            mode: "async".into(),
            personal: true,
            redaction_rules: vec![
                "author_request".into(),
                "private_info".into(),
            ],
            welcome: None,
            moderator: None,
        }
    }

    }
```

src/record.rs

```rust
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::Result;
use crate::proof::ProofOrArray;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const TIERS: [&str; 4] = ["observe", "communicate", "control", "terminal"];

pub fn tier_rank(t: &str) -> Option<u8> {
    TIERS.iter().position(|x| *x == t).map(|i| i as u8)
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Record {
    RoomCreated(RoomCreated),
    Message(Message),
    Join(Join),
    Leave(Leave),
    Redact(Redact),
    Refusal(Refusal),
    HumanOverride(HumanOverride),
    PortLanding(PortLanding),
    Bookmark(Bookmark),
    Presence(Presence),
}

impl Record {
    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> { crate::hash::hash_of(self) }
    pub fn seq(&self) -> u64 {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => 0,
            Record::Message(m) => m.seq,
            Record::Join(j) => j.seq,
            Record::Leave(l) => l.seq,
            Record::Redact(r) => r.seq,
            Record::Refusal(r) => r.seq,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => o.seq,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => p.seq,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => b.seq,
            Record::Presence(p) => p.seq,
        }
    }
    pub fn prev(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => None,
            Record::Message(m) => m.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Join(j) => j.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Leave(l) => l.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Redact(r) => r.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Refusal(r) => r.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => o.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::PortLanding(p) => p.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Bookmark(b) => b.prev.as_ref(),
            Record::Presence(p) => p.prev.as_ref(),
        }
    }
    pub fn proof(&self) -> &ProofOrArray {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::Message(m) => &m.proof,
            Record::Join(j) => &j.proof,
            Record::Leave(l) => &l.proof,
            Record::Redact(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::Refusal(r) => &r.proof,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => &o.proof,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => &p.proof,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => &b.proof,
            Record::Presence(p) => &p.proof,
        }
    }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey {
        match self {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::Message(m) => &m.world_id,
            Record::Join(j) => &j.world_id,
            Record::Leave(l) => &l.world_id,
            Record::Redact(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::Refusal(r) => &r.world_id,
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => &o.world_id,
            Record::PortLanding(p) => &p.world_id,
            Record::Bookmark(b) => &b.world_id,
            Record::Presence(p) => &p.world_id,
        }
    }
    pub fn author_id(&self) -> Option<&DidKey> {
        match self {
            Record::Message(m) => Some(&m.author_id),
            Record::Redact(r) => Some(&r.subject_id),
            Record::Join(j) => Some(&j.subject_id),
            Record::Leave(l) => Some(&l.subject_id),
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => Some(&o.overridden_by),
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => Some(&r.creator_id),
            Record::Refusal(_) => None,
            Record::PortLanding(_) => None,
            Record::Bookmark(_) => None,
            Record::Presence(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RoomCreated {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    pub creator_id: DidKey,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Message {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub author_id: DidKey,
    pub text: String,
    pub kind: String,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub in_reply_to: Option<String>,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Join {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub subject_id: DidKey,
    pub tier: String,
    pub is_ai: bool,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Leave {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub subject_id: DidKey,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Redact {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub subject_id: DidKey,
    pub target_hash: String,
    pub actor_role: String,   // "author" | "moderator" (| "keeper" future)
    pub rule: String,         // must be in template.redaction_rules
    pub reason: String,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Refusal {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub request_hash: String,
    pub requester_id: DidKey,
    pub reason: String,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct HumanOverride {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub overrides_hash: String,
    pub overridden_by: DidKey,
    pub action: String,   // "none" | "restore"
    pub reason: String,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PortLanding {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub transcript: crate::transcript::Transcript,
    pub intent: String,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub target_room: Option<DidKey>,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Bookmark {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub room_id: DidKey,
    pub note: String,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Presence {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub seq: u64,
    pub prev: Option<String>,
    pub subject_id: DidKey,
    pub rooms: Vec<DidKey>,
    pub ttl: u64,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

// ---- Constructors ----

impl RoomCreated {
    pub fn new(world_id: DidKey, template: RoomTemplate, creator_id: DidKey) -> Self {
        RoomCreated { world_id, template, creator_id, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }
    }
}
impl Message {
    pub fn new(world_id: DidKey, seq: u64, prev: Option<String>, author_id: DidKey, text: String, kind: String) -> Self {
        Message { world_id, seq, prev, author_id, text, kind, in_reply_to: None, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }
    }

impl Join {

        Join { world_id, seq, prev, subject_id, tier, is_ai, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }
    }
}
impl Leave {
    pub fn new(world_id: DidKey, seq: u64, prev: Option<String>, subject_id: DidKey) -> Self {
        Leave { world_id, seq, prev, subject_id, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }
    }
}
impl Redact {
    pub fn new(
        world_id: DidKey, seq: u64, prev: Option<String>,
        subject_id: DidKey, target_hash: String,
        actor_role: String, rule: String, reason: String,
    ) -> Self {
        Redact {
            world_id, seq, prev, subject_id, target_hash,
            actor_role, rule, reason,
            ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder(),
        }
    }
}
impl Refusal {
    pub fn new(world_id: DidKey, seq: u64, prev: Option<String>, request_hash: String, requester_id: DidKey, reason: String) -> Self {
        Refusal { world_id, seq, prev, request_hash, requester_id, reason, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }
    }
}
impl HumanOverride {
    pub fn new(world_id: DidKey, seq: u64, prev: Option<String>, overrides_hash: String, overridden_by: DidKey, action: String, reason: String) -> Self {
        HumanOverride { world_id, seq, prev, overrides_hash, overridden_by, action, reason, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }
    }

        PortLanding { world_id, seq, prev, transcript, intent, target_room, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }

        Bookmark { world_id, seq, prev, room_id, note, ts: crate::now(), proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder() }
```

src/room.rs

```rust
use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::record::*;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

fn short_hash(h: &str) -> String {
    h.chars().take(16).collect()
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Member {
    pub tier: String,
    pub is_ai: bool,
}

pub struct Room {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    keypair: Keypair,
    records: Vec<Record>,
    members: HashMap<DidKey, Member>,
}

impl Room {
    pub fn create(template: RoomTemplate, creator: Keypair) -> Result<Self> {
        let world_id = creator.did();
        let mut members = HashMap::new();
        members.insert(
            world_id.clone(),
            Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false },
        );
        let mut room = Room {
            world_id: world_id.clone(),
            template: template.clone(),
            keypair: creator,
            records: Vec::new(),
            members,
        };
        let rec = RoomCreated::new(world_id.clone(), template, world_id);
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &room.keypair, Record::RoomCreated, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        room.records.push(Record::RoomCreated(signed));
        Ok(room)
    }

    pub fn records(&self) -> &[Record] { &self.records }
    pub fn last_hash(&self) -> Option<String> { self.records.last().and_then(|r| r.hash().ok()) }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey { &self.world_id }
    pub fn template(&self) -> &RoomTemplate { &self.template }
    pub fn members(&self) -> &HashMap<DidKey, Member> { &self.members }
    pub fn keypair(&self) -> &Keypair { &self.keypair }

    /// A message is currently redacted iff it has at least one
    /// redact record that has not been resolved by a restore.
    pub fn is_redacted(&self, message_hash: &str) -> bool {
        let mut redact_hashes: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut resolved: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
        for rec in &self.records {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) if r.target_hash == message_hash => {
                    if let Ok(h) = rec.hash() { redact_hashes.push(h); }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    resolved.insert(o.overrides_hash.clone());
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        redact_hashes.iter().any(|h| !resolved.contains(h))
    }

    fn next_seq(&self) -> u64 { self.records.len() as u64 }
    pub(crate) fn push(&mut self, rec: Record) { self.records.push(rec); }

    fn refuse(&mut self, requester: DidKey, request_hash: String, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Refusal::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            request_hash, requester, reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.keypair, Record::Refusal, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Refusal(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn rate_ok(&self, author: &DidKey) -> bool {
        let window = self.template.rate_limit_window as usize;
        let cap = self.template.rate_limit_count as usize;
        let recent = self.records.iter().rev().take(window)
            .filter(|r| matches!(r, Record::Message(m) if &m.author_id == author))
            .count();
        recent < cap
    }

    fn inter_ai_pace_ok(&self, is_ai: bool) -> bool {
        if !is_ai || self.template.inter_ai_pace == 0 {
            return true;
        }
        let mut last_ai_idx: Option<usize> = None;
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate().rev() {
            if let Record::Message(m) = rec {
                let ai = self.members.get(&m.author_id).map(|x| x.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                if ai {
                    last_ai_idx = Some(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        match last_ai_idx {
            None => true,
            Some(idx) => {
                let since = (self.records.len() - idx - 1) as u64;
                since >= self.template.inter_ai_pace
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn join(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, tier: &str, is_ai: bool) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "already_member");
        }
        if tier_rank(tier).is_none() {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "unknown_tier");
        }
        if self.template.personal && subject != self.world_id {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "personal_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "human_only" && is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "human_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "ai_only" && !is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "ai_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "invite" && !self.template.personal {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "invite_required");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "approval" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "approval_required");
        }
        let rec = Join::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), tier.into(), is_ai,
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Join, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.insert(subject, Member { tier: tier.into(), is_ai });
        let out = Record::Join(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn leave(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair) -> Result<Option<Record>> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if !self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let rec = Leave::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(), subject.clone(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Leave, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.remove(&subject);
        let out = Record::Leave(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(Some(out))
    }

    pub fn post(&mut self, author_kp: &Keypair, text: &str, kind: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let author = author_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&author).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "insufficient_tier");
        }
        if text.len() as u64 > self.template.max_msg_len {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "message_too_long");
        }
        if !self.rate_ok(&author) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "rate_limit");
        }
        if !self.inter_ai_pace_ok(m.is_ai) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "inter_ai_pace");
        }
        let rec = Message::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            author.clone(), text.into(), kind.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, author_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Message, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Message(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn redact(
        &mut self,
        subject_kp: &Keypair,
        target_hash: &str,
        rule: &str,
        reason: &str,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&subject).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(target_hash));
        let target_msg = match target {
            Some(Record::Message(msg)) => msg,
            _ => return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "target_not_found"),
        };
        if self.template.moderation == "none" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "moderation_disabled");
        }
        let is_author = target_msg.author_id == subject;
        let is_mod = self.template.moderation == "moderator"
            && tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
        let actor_role = if is_author {
            "author"
        } else if is_mod {
            "moderator"
        } else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_allowed");
        };
        if !self.template.redaction_rules.iter().any(|r| r == rule) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "rule_not_allowed");
        }
        let rec = Redact::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), target_hash.into(),
            actor_role.into(), rule.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Redact, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Redact(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn override_ai(&mut self, overrider_kp: &Keypair, overrides_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let overrider = overrider_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&overrider).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("overrider is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can override".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(overrides_hash));
        let Some(target_rec) = target else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target not found".into()));
        };
        let Some(author) = target_rec.author_id() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target has no author".into()));
        };
        let is_ai = self.members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
        if !is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("can only override AI records".into()));
        }
        if let Some(spec) = &self.template.moderator {
            if &spec.moderator_id == author && !spec.can_be_overridden {
                return Err(Error::Policy("this AI cannot be overridden".into()));
            }
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            overrides_hash.into(), overrider.clone(), "none".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, overrider_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Restore a redacted message by naming the incident (the
    /// redact record's hash). After this, the message's text is
    /// visible again; the redact record stays in the log.
    pub fn restore_incident(&mut self, restorer_kp: &Keypair, redact_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let restorer = restorer_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&restorer).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("restorer is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can restore".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(redact_hash));
        match target {
            Some(Record::Redact(_)) => {}
            _ => return Err(Error::Policy("restore target must be a redact record".into())),
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            redact_hash.into(), restorer.clone(), "restore".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, restorer_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn visible_log(&self) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
        use serde_json::json;

        // Pre-compute hashes; index by hash for lookups.
        let hashes: Vec<String> = self.records.iter()
            .map(|r| r.hash().unwrap_or_default())
            .collect();
        let mut hash_to_idx: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        for (i, h) in hashes.iter().enumerate() {
            hash_to_idx.insert(h.clone(), i);
        }

        // Pass 1: redactions per target (by idx) and restored redacts (by idx).
        let mut redactions_by_target: HashMap<usize, Vec<usize>> = HashMap::new();
        let mut resolved_redacts: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    if let Some(&target_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&r.target_hash) {
                        redactions_by_target.entry(target_idx).or_default().push(i);
                    }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    if let Some(&redact_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&o.overrides_hash) {
                        resolved_redacts.insert(redact_idx);
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        // Pass 2: build the view.
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Message(m) => {
                    let mut still_redacted: Option<usize> = None;
                    let mut restored: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
                    if let Some(redact_idxs) = redactions_by_target.get(&i) {
                        for &ri in redact_idxs {
                            if resolved_redacts.contains(&ri) {
                                restored.push(ri);
                            } else if still_redacted.is_none() {
                                still_redacted = Some(ri);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if let Some(ri) = still_redacted {
                        if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                            out.push(json!({
                                "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                                "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                                "text": "[removed]",
                                "redacted": true,
                                "reason": r.reason,
                                "rule": r.rule,
                                "incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]),
                                "ts": m.ts,
                            }));
                        }
                    } else {
                        let mut obj = json!({
                            "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                            "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                            "text": m.text,
                            "redacted": false,
                            "ts": m.ts,
                        });
                        if !restored.is_empty() {
                            let notes: Vec<serde_json::Value> = restored.iter().map(|&ri| {
                                if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                                    json!({
                                        "incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]),
                                        "reason": r.reason,
                                        "rule": r.rule,
                                    })
                                } else {
                                    json!({})
                                }
                            }).collect();
                            obj["restored_incidents"] = json!(notes);
                        }
                        out.push(obj);
                    }
                }
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({
                        "seq": r.seq, "type": "redact",
                        "by": r.subject_id.as_str(),
                        "reason": r.reason,
                        "target": r.target_hash,
                        "rule": r.rule,
                        "actor_role": r.actor_role,
                        "incident": short_hash(&hashes[i]),
                    }));
                }
                Record::Join(j) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": j.seq, "type": "join", "subject": j.subject_id.as_str(), "tier": j.tier, "is_ai": j.is_ai}));
                }
                Record::Leave(l) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": l.seq, "type": "leave", "subject": l.subject_id.as_str()}));
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": o.seq, "type": "human_override", "by": o.overridden_by.as_str(), "overrides": o.overrides_hash, "action": o.action, "reason": o.reason}));
                }
                Record::Refusal(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": r.seq, "type": "refused", "requester": r.requester_id.as_str(), "reason": r.reason}));
                }
                Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": 0, "type": "created"}));
                }
                Record::PortLanding(_) | Record::Bookmark(_) | Record::Presence(_) => {}
            }
        }
        out
    }
}
```

src/verify.rs

```rust
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::record::*;
use crate::room::{Member, Room};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

/// Full offline verification: chain, signatures, and policy replay.
pub fn verify_room(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let world_id = room.world_id();
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    let mut members: HashMap<DidKey, Member> = HashMap::new();
    let mut redacted: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();

    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                if i != 0 {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: room_created not at start")));
                }
            }
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }

        let body = hash::body_for_signing(rec)?;
        let proofs = rec.proof();

        if !matches!(rec, Record::Leave(_)) {
            let Some(rp) = proofs.find_by_method(world_id) else {
                return Err(Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof")));
            };
            rp.verify(&body)?;
        }

        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => {
                let creator_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.creator_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing creator proof")))?;
                creator_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(r.creator_id.clone(), Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false });
            }
            Record::Message(m) => {
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&m.author_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author not a member")));
                };
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author tier insufficient")));
                }
                let author_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&m.author_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing author proof")))?;
                author_proof.verify(&body)?;
            }
            Record::Join(j) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&j.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(j.subject_id.clone(), Member { tier: j.tier.clone(), is_ai: j.is_ai });
            }
            Record::Leave(l) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&l.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.remove(&l.subject_id);
            }
            Record::Redact(r) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&r.subject_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redactor not a member")));
                };
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&r.target_hash));
                let target_msg = match target {
                    Some(Record::Message(m)) => m,
                    _ => return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redact target not a message"))),
                };
                if room.template().moderation == "none" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: moderation disabled")));
                }
                let is_author = target_msg.author_id == r.subject_id;
                let is_mod = room.template().moderation == "moderator"
                    && tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
                let role_ok = match r.actor_role.as_str() {
                    "author" => is_author,
                    "moderator" => is_mod,
                    _ => false,
                };
                if !role_ok {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: actor_role {:?} not permitted (is_author={is_author}, is_mod={is_mod})",
                        r.actor_role
                    )));
                }
                if !room.template().redaction_rules.iter().any(|x| x == &r.rule) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: rule {:?} not in template's redaction_rules",
                        r.rule
                    )));
                }
                redacted.insert(r.target_hash.clone());
            }
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                let overrider_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&o.overridden_by)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing overrider proof")))?;
                overrider_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&o.overridden_by) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider not a member")));
                };
                if mem.is_ai {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: only humans can override")));
                }
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider tier insufficient")));
                }
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&o.overrides_hash));
                let Some(target) = target else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override target not found")));
                };
                match o.action.as_str() {
                    "none" => {
                        let Some(author) = target.author_id() else {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target has no author")));
                        };
                        let target_is_ai = members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                        if !target_is_ai {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target must be AI-authored")));
                        }
                    }
                    "restore" => {
                        if !matches!(target, Record::Redact(_)) {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: restore target must be a redact record")));
                        }
                    }
                    other => {
                        return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown override action {other:?}")));
                    }
                }
            }
            Record::Refusal(_) => {}
            Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: port_landing only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                crate::transcript::verify_transcript(&p.transcript)
                    .map_err(|e| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: transcript: {e}")))?;
                if p.intent != "journal" && p.intent != "room" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown port intent")));
                }
                if p.intent == "room" && p.target_room.is_none() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: intent 'room' without target_room")));
                }
            }
            Record::Bookmark(_) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: bookmark only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
            }
            Record::Presence(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: presence only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                if p.subject_id != *world_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: presence subject must be the room's key")));
                }
            }
        }

        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }

    if room.template().personal {
        for rec in room.records() {
            if let Record::Join(j) = rec {
                if &j.subject_id != world_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy("personal room contains non-seed member".into()));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// Chain integrity only — useful when signatures are known-good.
pub fn verify_chain(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) if i != 0 => {
                return Err(Error::Chain("room_created not at start".into()));
            }
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {}
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }
        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

tests/integration.rs

```rust
use chat_world::commons::{verify_commons, Commons};
use chat_world::home::HomeRoom;
use chat_world::transcript::{sign_transcript, verify_transcript, TranscriptEntry};
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn make_room() -> Room {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    Room::create(template, alice).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn room_verifies_after_activity() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "hello", "normal")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn non_member_is_refused() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let rec = room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "not_a_member"),
        _ => panic!("expected refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn rate_limit_enforced() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.rate_limit_count = 2;
    room.template.rate_limit_window = 5;
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "one", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "two", "normal")?;
    let rec = room.post(&bob, "three", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "rate_limit"),
        _ => panic!("expected rate-limit refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn inter_ai_pace_fires() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.inter_ai_pace = 1;
    let ai_a = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_b = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&ai_a, "communicate", true)?;
    room.join(&ai_b, "communicate", true)?;
    room.post(&ai_a, "first", "normal")?;
    let rec = room.post(&ai_b, "second", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "inter_ai_pace"),
        _ => panic!("expected inter_ai_pace refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn author_can_redact_own() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "oops", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&bob, &h, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    match r {
        Record::Redact(_) => {}
        _ => panic!("expected redact"),
    }
    assert!(room.is_redacted(&h));
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn moderator_can_redact_other() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let moderator = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&moderator, "control", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "spam", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    room.redact(&moderator, &h, "spam", "spam")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn non_moderator_cannot_redact_other() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&alice, &h, "spam", "no")?;
    match r {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "not_allowed"),
        _ => panic!("expected refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn redact_rejects_unknown_rule() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "text", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&bob, &h, "made_up_rule", "no")?;
    match r {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "rule_not_allowed"),
        _ => panic!("expected rule refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn restore_makes_text_visible() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    // Before restore: visible_log shows [removed]
    let before = room.visible_log();
    let view = before.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message") && v["redacted"].as_bool() == Some(true))
        .expect("no redacted message in view");
    assert_eq!(view["text"].as_str(), Some("[removed]"));
    assert!(view["incident"].as_str().is_some());
    assert!(view["rule"].as_str().is_some());
    assert!(view["reason"].as_str().is_some());

    // Dave (control) restores
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    // After restore: text visible, with restore note
    let after = room.visible_log();
    let view = after.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message")
              && v["text"].as_str() == Some("words I take back"))
        .expect("message not visible after restore");
    assert!(view["restored_incidents"].is_array());
    let restores = view["restored_incidents"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(restores.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(
        restores[0]["incident"].as_str(),
        Some(&redact_hash[..16])
    );

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn redacted_record_unchanged_after_restore() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    // The data layer: the original message record is unchanged.
    let stored = room.records().iter()
        .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&msg_hash))
        .expect("original message not found");
    match stored {
        Record::Message(m) => assert_eq!(m.text, "words I take back"),
        _ => panic!("original record was not a message"),
    }

    // And the room still verifies.
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn restore_rejects_non_redact_target() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "hello", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    assert!(room.restore_incident(&dave, &msg_hash, "test").is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]

}

#[test]
```
