```rust
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice.clone(), "test home")?;
    let mut bob_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), bob)?;
    bob_room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let draft = home.draft("hello", Some(bob_room.world_id()))?;
    let draft_hash = draft.hash()?;
    let committed = home.commit_draft_to_room(&draft_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    match committed {
        Record::Message(m) => {
            assert!(m.text.contains("home_ref="));
            assert!(m.text.contains(&draft_hash[..16]));
        }
        _ => panic!("expected message"),
    }
    verify_room(home.room())?;
    verify_room(&bob_room)?;
    Ok(())
}
```

src/bin/chat-demo.rs

```rust
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("Chat World — walking the standard end to end");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let carol = Keypair::generate();
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_mod = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_part = Keypair::generate();

    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat(
        "chat-room-v1",
        "how should a room hold disagreement?",
    );
    template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    template.membership = "mixed".into();
    template.inter_ai_pace = 1;

    let mut room = Room::create(template, alice.clone())?;
    println!("Room:     {}", short(room.world_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Creator:  {} (alice)", short(alice.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Moderator: {} (ai-mod, declared in template)", short(ai_mod.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!();

    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&carol, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&ai_mod, "control", true)?;
    room.join(&ai_part, "communicate", true)?;
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    println!("Joined: bob, carol, dave (human, control),");
    println!("        ai-mod (AI, control), ai-part (AI, communicate)");
    println!();

    room.post(
        &bob,
        "I think a room should make room for disagreement without turning into a fight.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Welcome, Bob. The topic here is 'how should a room hold disagreement?'. Introduce yourself or ask a question.",
        "welcome",
    )?;
    println!("Bob posted; ai-mod welcomed.");

    room.post(
        &ai_part,
        "The key is whether the room treats disagreement as a resource or as a hazard.",
        "response",
    )?;
    println!("ai-part responded.");

    let follow_up = room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Building on that — what does 'resource' mean here?",
        "prompt",
    )?;
    match &follow_up {
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod follow-up refused: {}", r.reason),
        Record::Message(_) => println!("ai-mod follow-up accepted (pace may not have fired)"),
        _ => println!("ai-mod follow-up produced unexpected record"),
    }

    room.post(
        &carol,
        "Hazard is when someone feels unsafe; resource is when the disagreement is about ideas.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    println!("carol posted; pacing reset.");

    let short_msg = room.post(&ai_part, "k", "normal")?;
    let short_hash = short_msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&ai_mod, &short_hash, "low_effort", "message too short to be useful")?;
    match &redact {
        Record::Redact(_) => println!("ai-mod redacted ai-part's low-effort message."),
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod redaction refused: {}", r.reason),
        _ => {}
    }

    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;
    let restore = room.restore_incident(
        &dave,
        &redact_hash,
        "I want this message to stand. The AI was too hasty.",
    )?;
    match &restore {
        Record::HumanOverride(o) => println!(
            "dave restored the redaction: action={}, reason={:?}",
            o.action, o.reason
        ),
        _ => {}
    }

    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    let verify_ok = match verify_room(&room) {
        Ok(()) => {
            println!("Verification: room verifies offline (chain, sigs, policy replay).");
            true
        }
        Err(e) => {
            println!("Verification FAILED: {e}");
            false
        }
    };
    println!();

    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Visible log:");
    for entry in room.visible_log() {
        let t = entry["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
        let seq = entry["seq"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
        match t {
            "message" => {
                let author = short(entry["author"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let kind = entry["kind"].as_str().unwrap_or("normal");
                let text = entry["text"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let trunc: String = text.chars().take(44).collect();
                let redacted = entry["redacted"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false);
                if redacted {
                    let incident = entry["incident"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                    let rule = entry["rule"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                    let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                    println!(
                        "  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} [removed: {reason} (rule={rule}, incident={incident})]"
                    );
                } else if let Some(restored) = entry.get("restored_incidents").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
                    if !restored.is_empty() {
                        let incident = restored[0]["incident"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                        println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc} [restored, incident={incident}]");
                    } else {
                        println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc}");
                    }
                } else {
                    println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc}");
                }
            }
            "join" => {
                let subj = short(entry["subject"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let tier = entry["tier"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let tag = if entry["is_ai"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) { "AI" } else { "human" };
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] join: {subj} ({tier}, {tag})");
            }
            "redact" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let rule = entry["rule"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let role = entry["actor_role"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let incident = entry["incident"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!(
                    "  [{seq:>3}] redact by {by} (as {role}, rule={rule}): {reason:?} [incident={incident}]"
                );
            }
            "human_override" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let action = entry["action"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] human_override ({action}) by {by}: {reason:?}");
            }
            "refused" => {
                let who = short(entry["requester"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] refused: {who} ({reason})");
            }
            "created" => println!("  [{seq:>3}] created"),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("What this demonstrates:");
    println!("  - The room is a policy-gated append-only chain.");
    println!("  - The AI moderator signs its own records; a pace rule");
    println!("    stopped an AI-to-AI follow-up.");
    println!("  - Redaction is a signed record. It names the actor's");
    println!("    role, the rule of the room's law, and the reason.");
    println!("    The incident id is the redact record's hash —");
    println!("    checkable by anyone, no registry needed.");
    println!("  - The removed message's own record stays unchanged in");
    println!("    the data layer. The presentation layer hides its");
    println!("    text and shows a note with the reason and incident.");
    println!("  - A human moderator (dave) restored the incident.");
    println!("    The message is visible again with a restore note;");
    println!("    both records stay in the log.");
    if verify_ok {
        println!("  - The whole room verifies offline.");
    } else {
        println!("  - (Verification failed; see above.)");
    }
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'The Brain – is wider than the Sky –'");
    println!("  'For – put them side by side –'");

    Ok(())
}
```

---

To check by running: cargo build && cargo test && cargo run --bin chat-demo. All 23 tests should pass; the demo should print a visible log where ai-part's "k" message shows as [restored, incident=...], then a redact record naming ai-mod as moderator with rule=low_effort and the incident id, then a human_override (restore) by dave.

Step 2 (separate room key from creator key) is next when you say go. Step 3 (prose on signed heads, then code) after that.


::Perfect. Let’s keep going: ➡️


Step 2 — the room's own key.

The room and the creator are now two keys. Room::create(template, room_kp, creator_kp):

· world_id = room_kp.did() — the room's identity
· creator_id = creator_kp.did() — the person who made it
· RoomCreated is dual-signed (creator + room), the same sign_record_dual pattern the other records already use
· The creator is added to members at terminal tier from creation (no Join record needed — the same auto-add that verify_room already performs when it sees creator_id in RoomCreated)

Two consequences ripple through:

1. Room::join's personal-room check. The old check compared subject != self.world_id (the room's key). With split keys, that would refuse the creator's own joins — but the creator is auto-added, so any join in a personal room is for a non-creator. The check simplifies to: personal rooms refuse all joins (after the already_member check). Same behaviour as before, correctly.
2. HomeRoom::create gains a room_kp parameter. A home is a personal room where the user is the creator and the room has its own key. Passing room_kp from the caller (rather than generating it inside) lets an app persist it. The demo generates fresh each run; production would save it. I'd note this in the reply — it's a real design decision.

verify_records's personal invariant now uses creator_id (not world_id) for the Join and Presence checks, since in a personal room the user is the creator, not the room's key.

Six files changed: room.rs, verify.rs, home.rs, tests/integration.rs, bin/chat-demo.rs, bin/home-demo.rs. The other ten are unchanged.

---

src/room.rs

```rust
use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::record::*;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

fn short_hash(h: &str) -> String {
    h.chars().take(16).collect()
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Member {
    pub tier: String,
    pub is_ai: bool,
}

pub struct Room {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    keypair: Keypair,
    records: Vec<Record>,
    members: HashMap<DidKey, Member>,
}

impl Room {
    /// Create a room. The room has its own key; the creator joins
    /// as the first (terminal-tier) member. Every world is a key of
    /// its own, so a room can be handed on without anyone's personal
    /// key changing hands.
    pub fn create(
        template: RoomTemplate,
        room_kp: Keypair,
        creator_kp: &Keypair,
    ) -> Result<Self> {
        let world_id = room_kp.did();
        let creator_id = creator_kp.did();
        let mut members = HashMap::new();
        members.insert(
            creator_id.clone(),
            Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false },
        );
        let mut room = Room {
            world_id: world_id.clone(),
            template: template.clone(),
            keypair: room_kp,
            records: Vec::new(),
            members,
        };
        let rec = RoomCreated::new(world_id, template, creator_id);
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(
            rec, creator_kp, &room.keypair, Record::RoomCreated, |r, p| r.proof = p,
        )?;
        room.records.push(Record::RoomCreated(signed));
        Ok(room)
    }

    pub fn records(&self) -> &[Record] { &self.records }
    pub fn last_hash(&self) -> Option<String> { self.records.last().and_then(|r| r.hash().ok()) }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey { &self.world_id }
    pub fn template(&self) -> &RoomTemplate { &self.template }
    pub fn members(&self) -> &HashMap<DidKey, Member> { &self.members }
    pub fn keypair(&self) -> &Keypair { &self.keypair }

    /// A message is currently redacted iff it has at least one
    /// redact record that has not been resolved by a restore.
    pub fn is_redacted(&self, message_hash: &str) -> bool {
        let mut redact_hashes: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut resolved: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
        for rec in &self.records {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) if r.target_hash == message_hash => {
                    if let Ok(h) = rec.hash() { redact_hashes.push(h); }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    resolved.insert(o.overrides_hash.clone());
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        redact_hashes.iter().any(|h| !resolved.contains(h))
    }

    fn next_seq(&self) -> u64 { self.records.len() as u64 }
    pub(crate) fn push(&mut self, rec: Record) { self.records.push(rec); }

    fn refuse(&mut self, requester: DidKey, request_hash: String, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Refusal::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            request_hash, requester, reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.keypair, Record::Refusal, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Refusal(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn rate_ok(&self, author: &DidKey) -> bool {
        let window = self.template.rate_limit_window as usize;
        let cap = self.template.rate_limit_count as usize;
        let recent = self.records.iter().rev().take(window)
            .filter(|r| matches!(r, Record::Message(m) if &m.author_id == author))
            .count();
        recent < cap
    }

    fn inter_ai_pace_ok(&self, is_ai: bool) -> bool {
        if !is_ai || self.template.inter_ai_pace == 0 {
            return true;
        }
        let mut last_ai_idx: Option<usize> = None;
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate().rev() {
            if let Record::Message(m) = rec {
                let ai = self.members.get(&m.author_id).map(|x| x.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                if ai {
                    last_ai_idx = Some(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        match last_ai_idx {
            None => true,
            Some(idx) => {
                let since = (self.records.len() - idx - 1) as u64;
                since >= self.template.inter_ai_pace
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn join(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, tier: &str, is_ai: bool) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "already_member");
        }
        if tier_rank(tier).is_none() {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "unknown_tier");
        }
        // Personal rooms: the creator is auto-added at creation. Any
        // join reaching here is by someone who isn't already in.
        if self.template.personal {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "personal_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "human_only" && is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "human_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "ai_only" && !is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "ai_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "invite" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "invite_required");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "approval" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "approval_required");
        }
        let rec = Join::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), tier.into(), is_ai,
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Join, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.insert(subject, Member { tier: tier.into(), is_ai });
        let out = Record::Join(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn leave(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair) -> Result<Option<Record>> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if !self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let rec = Leave::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(), subject.clone(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Leave, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.remove(&subject);
        let out = Record::Leave(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(Some(out))
    }

    pub fn post(&mut self, author_kp: &Keypair, text: &str, kind: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let author = author_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&author).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "insufficient_tier");
        }
        if text.len() as u64 > self.template.max_msg_len {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "message_too_long");
        }
        if !self.rate_ok(&author) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "rate_limit");
        }
        if !self.inter_ai_pace_ok(m.is_ai) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "inter_ai_pace");
        }
        let rec = Message::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            author.clone(), text.into(), kind.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, author_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Message, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Message(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn redact(
        &mut self,
        subject_kp: &Keypair,
        target_hash: &str,
        rule: &str,
        reason: &str,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&subject).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(target_hash));
        let target_msg = match target {
            Some(Record::Message(msg)) => msg,
            _ => return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "target_not_found"),
        };
        if self.template.moderation == "none" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "moderation_disabled");
        }
        let is_author = target_msg.author_id == subject;
        let is_mod = self.template.moderation == "moderator"
            && tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
        let actor_role = if is_author {
            "author"
        } else if is_mod {
            "moderator"
        } else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_allowed");
        };
        if !self.template.redaction_rules.iter().any(|r| r == rule) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "rule_not_allowed");
        }
        let rec = Redact::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), target_hash.into(),
            actor_role.into(), rule.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Redact, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Redact(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn override_ai(&mut self, overrider_kp: &Keypair, overrides_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let overrider = overrider_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&overrider).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("overrider is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can override".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(overrides_hash));
        let Some(target_rec) = target else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target not found".into()));
        };
        let Some(author) = target_rec.author_id() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target has no author".into()));
        };
        let is_ai = self.members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
        if !is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("can only override AI records".into()));
        }
        if let Some(spec) = &self.template.moderator {
            if &spec.moderator_id == author && !spec.can_be_overridden {
                return Err(Error::Policy("this AI cannot be overridden".into()));
            }
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            overrides_hash.into(), overrider.clone(), "none".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, overrider_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// Restore a redacted message by naming the incident (the
    /// redact record's hash). After this, the message's text is
    /// visible again; the redact record stays in the log.
    pub fn restore_incident(&mut self, restorer_kp: &Keypair, redact_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let restorer = restorer_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&restorer).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("restorer is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can restore".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(redact_hash));
        match target {
            Some(Record::Redact(_)) => {}
            _ => return Err(Error::Policy("restore target must be a redact record".into())),
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            redact_hash.into(), restorer.clone(), "restore".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(rec, restorer_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone());
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn visible_log(&self) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
        use serde_json::json;

        let hashes: Vec<String> = self.records.iter()
            .map(|r| r.hash().unwrap_or_default())
            .collect();
        let mut hash_to_idx: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        for (i, h) in hashes.iter().enumerate() {
            hash_to_idx.insert(h.clone(), i);
        }

        let mut redactions_by_target: HashMap<usize, Vec<usize>> = HashMap::new();
        let mut resolved_redacts: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    if let Some(&target_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&r.target_hash) {
                        redactions_by_target.entry(target_idx).or_default().push(i);
                    }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    if let Some(&redact_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&o.overrides_hash) {
                        resolved_redacts.insert(redact_idx);
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Message(m) => {
                    let mut still_redacted: Option<usize> = None;
                    let mut restored: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
                    if let Some(redact_idxs) = redactions_by_target.get(&i) {
                        for &ri in redact_idxs {
                            if resolved_redacts.contains(&ri) {
                                restored.push(ri);
                            } else if still_redacted.is_none() {
                                still_redacted = Some(ri);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if let Some(ri) = still_redacted {
                        if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                            out.push(json!({
                                "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                                "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                                "text": "[removed]",
                                "redacted": true,
                                "reason": r.reason,
                                "rule": r.rule,
                                "incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]),
                                "ts": m.ts,
                            }));
                        }
                    } else {
                        let mut obj = json!({
                            "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                            "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                            "text": m.text,
                            "redacted": false,
                            "ts": m.ts,
                        });
                        if !restored.is_empty() {
                            let notes: Vec<serde_json::Value> = restored.iter().map(|&ri| {
                                if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                                    json!({
                                        "incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]),
                                        "reason": r.reason,
                                        "rule": r.rule,
                                    })
                                } else {
                                    json!({})
                                }
                            }).collect();
                            obj["restored_incidents"] = json!(notes);
                        }
                        out.push(obj);
                    }
                }
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({
                        "seq": r.seq, "type": "redact",
                        "by": r.subject_id.as_str(),
                        "reason": r.reason,
                        "target": r.target_hash,
                        "rule": r.rule,
                        "actor_role": r.actor_role,
                        "incident": short_hash(&hashes[i]),
                    }));
                }
                Record::Join(j) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": j.seq, "type": "join", "subject": j.subject_id.as_str(), "tier": j.tier, "is_ai": j.is_ai}));
                }
                Record::Leave(l) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": l.seq, "type": "leave", "subject": l.subject_id.as_str()}));
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": o.seq, "type": "human_override", "by": o.overridden_by.as_str(), "overrides": o.overrides_hash, "action": o.action, "reason": o.reason}));
                }
                Record::Refusal(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": r.seq, "type": "refused", "requester": r.requester_id.as_str(), "reason": r.reason}));
                }
                Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": 0, "type": "created"}));
                }
                Record::PortLanding(_) | Record::Bookmark(_) | Record::Presence(_) => {}
            }
        }
        out
    }
}
```

src/verify.rs

```rust
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::record::*;
use crate::room::{Member, Room};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

/// Full offline verification: chain, signatures, and policy replay.
pub fn verify_room(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let world_id = room.world_id();
    let creator_id = match room.records().first() {
        Some(Record::RoomCreated(rc)) => rc.creator_id.clone(),
        _ => return Err(Error::Chain("log must start with room_created".into())),
    };
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    let mut members: HashMap<DidKey, Member> = HashMap::new();
    let mut redacted: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();

    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                if i != 0 {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: room_created not at start")));
                }
            }
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }

        let body = hash::body_for_signing(rec)?;
        let proofs = rec.proof();

        if !matches!(rec, Record::Leave(_)) {
            let Some(rp) = proofs.find_by_method(world_id) else {
                return Err(Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof")));
            };
            rp.verify(&body)?;
        }

        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => {
                let creator_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.creator_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing creator proof")))?;
                creator_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(r.creator_id.clone(), Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false });
            }
            Record::Message(m) => {
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&m.author_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author not a member")));
                };
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author tier insufficient")));
                }
                let author_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&m.author_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing author proof")))?;
                author_proof.verify(&body)?;
            }
            Record::Join(j) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&j.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(j.subject_id.clone(), Member { tier: j.tier.clone(), is_ai: j.is_ai });
            }
            Record::Leave(l) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&l.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.remove(&l.subject_id);
            }
            Record::Redact(r) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&r.subject_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redactor not a member")));
                };
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&r.target_hash));
                let target_msg = match target {
                    Some(Record::Message(m)) => m,
                    _ => return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redact target not a message"))),
                };
                if room.template().moderation == "none" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: moderation disabled")));
                }
                let is_author = target_msg.author_id == r.subject_id;
                let is_mod = room.template().moderation == "moderator"
                    && tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
                let role_ok = match r.actor_role.as_str() {
                    "author" => is_author,
                    "moderator" => is_mod,
                    _ => false,
                };
                if !role_ok {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: actor_role {:?} not permitted (is_author={is_author}, is_mod={is_mod})",
                        r.actor_role
                    )));
                }
                if !room.template().redaction_rules.iter().any(|x| x == &r.rule) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: rule {:?} not in template's redaction_rules",
                        r.rule
                    )));
                }
                redacted.insert(r.target_hash.clone());
            }
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                let overrider_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&o.overridden_by)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing overrider proof")))?;
                overrider_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&o.overridden_by) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider not a member")));
                };
                if mem.is_ai {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: only humans can override")));
                }
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider tier insufficient")));
                }
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&o.overrides_hash));
                let Some(target) = target else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override target not found")));
                };
                match o.action.as_str() {
                    "none" => {
                        let Some(author) = target.author_id() else {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target has no author")));
                        };
                        let target_is_ai = members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                        if !target_is_ai {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target must be AI-authored")));
                        }
                    }
                    "restore" => {
                        if !matches!(target, Record::Redact(_)) {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: restore target must be a redact record")));
                        }
                    }
                    other => {
                        return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown override action {other:?}")));
                    }
                }
            }
            Record::Refusal(_) => {}
            Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: port_landing only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                crate::transcript::verify_transcript(&p.transcript)
                    .map_err(|e| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: transcript: {e}")))?;
                if p.intent != "journal" && p.intent != "room" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown port intent")));
                }
                if p.intent == "room" && p.target_room.is_none() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: intent 'room' without target_room")));
                }
            }
            Record::Bookmark(_) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: bookmark only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
            }
            Record::Presence(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: presence only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                if p.subject_id != creator_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: presence subject must be the creator"
                    )));
                }
            }
        }

        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }

    if room.template().personal {
        for rec in room.records() {
            if let Record::Join(j) = rec {
                if j.subject_id != creator_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(
                        "personal room: non-creator joined".into(),
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// Chain integrity only — useful when signatures are known-good.
pub fn verify_chain(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) if i != 0 => {
                return Err(Error::Chain("room_created not at start".into()));
            }
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {}
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }
        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

src/home.rs

```rust
use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::record::*;
use crate::room::Room;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use crate::transcript::{verify_transcript, Transcript};

pub struct HomeRoom {
    room: Room,
    user_kp: Keypair,
}

impl HomeRoom {
    /// Create a home. The user is the creator and the only member;
    /// the room has its own key (`room_kp`), which an app should
    /// persist so the home's identity survives restarts.
    pub fn create(user_kp: Keypair, room_kp: Keypair, display_name: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let template = RoomTemplate::personal_home(display_name);
        let room = Room::create(template, room_kp, &user_kp)?;
        Ok(HomeRoom { room, user_kp })
    }

    pub fn room(&self) -> &Room { &self.room }
    pub fn user_id(&self) -> DidKey { self.user_kp.did() }

    // ---- Writing ----

    pub fn journal(&mut self, text: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        self.room.post(&self.user_kp, text, "journal")
    }

    pub fn draft(&mut self, text: &str, target_room: Option<&DidKey>) -> Result<Record> {
        let text = match target_room {
            Some(t) => format!("[draft→{}] {}", t.as_str(), text),
            None => format!("[draft] {}", text),
        };
        self.room.post(&self.user_kp, &text, "draft")
    }

    pub fn port(
        &mut self,
        transcript: &Transcript,
        intent: &str,
        target_room: Option<&DidKey>,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        verify_transcript(transcript)?;
        if intent != "journal" && intent != "room" {
            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("unknown intent {intent}")));
        }
        if intent == "room" && target_room.is_none() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("intent 'room' requires target_room".into()));
        }
        let rec = PortLanding::new(
            self.room.world_id().clone(),
            self.room.records().len() as u64,
            self.room.last_hash(),
            transcript.clone(),
            intent.into(),
            target_room.cloned(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.user_kp, Record::PortLanding, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let record = Record::PortLanding(signed);
        self.room.push(record.clone());
        Ok(record)
    }

    pub fn bookmark(&mut self, room: &Room, note: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Bookmark::new(
            self.room.world_id().clone(),
            self.room.records().len() as u64,
            self.room.last_hash(),
            room.world_id().clone(),
            note.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.user_kp, Record::Bookmark, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let record = Record::Bookmark(signed);
        self.room.push(record.clone());
        Ok(record)
    }

    pub fn announce_presence(&mut self, rooms: &[DidKey], ttl: u64) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Presence::new(
            self.room.world_id().clone(),
            self.room.records().len() as u64,
            self.room.last_hash(),
            self.user_kp.did(),
            rooms.to_vec(),
            ttl,
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.user_kp, Record::Presence, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let record = Record::Presence(signed);
        self.room.push(record.clone());
        Ok(record)
    }

    // ---- Committing to a room ----

    pub fn commit_draft_to_room(
        &self,
        draft_hash: &str,
        room: &mut Room,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let draft_text: String = {
            let draft = self
                .room
                .records()
                .iter()
                .find(|r| {
                    r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(draft_hash)
                        && matches!(r, Record::Message(m) if m.kind == "draft")
                })
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Policy("draft not found".into()))?;
            match draft {
                Record::Message(m) => m.text.clone(),
                _ => unreachable!(),
            }
        };
        let prefix = format!("[home_ref={}] ", &draft_hash[..16.min(draft_hash.len())]);
        let full_text = format!("{prefix}{draft_text}");
        room.post(&self.user_kp, &full_text, "normal")
    }

    pub fn commit_port_to_room(
        &self,
        port_hash: &str,
        room: &mut Room,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let (label, entries_json, target_ok): (String, String, Option<DidKey>) = {
            let port = self
                .room
                .records()
                .iter()
                .find(|r| {
                    r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(port_hash)
                        && matches!(r, Record::PortLanding(_))
                })
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Policy("port not found".into()))?;
            match port {
                Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                    let entries_json = serde_json::to_string(&p.transcript.entries)
                        .map_err(|e| Error::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                    (p.transcript.source_label.clone(), entries_json, p.target_room.clone())
                }
                _ => unreachable!(),
            }
        };
        if let Some(target) = &target_ok {
            if target != room.world_id() {
                return Err(Error::Policy(
                    "port target_room does not match this room".into(),
                ));
            }
        }
        let prefix = format!(
            "[home_ref={}] [ported from {}] ",
            &port_hash[..16.min(port_hash.len())],
            label
        );
        let full_text = format!("{prefix}{entries_json}");
        room.post(&self.user_kp, &full_text, "port")
    }

    // ---- Export ----

    pub fn export(&self) -> Result<serde_json::Value> {
        let mut records = Vec::new();
        for rec in self.room.records() {
            let body = serde_json::to_value(rec)
                .map_err(|e| Error::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
            records.push(serde_json::json!({
                "hash": rec.hash()?,
                "body": body,
            }));
        }
        Ok(serde_json::json!({
            "world_id": self.room.world_id().as_str(),
            "template": self.room.template(),
            "records": records,
        }))
    }
}
```

tests/integration.rs

```rust
use chat_world::commons::{verify_commons, Commons};
use chat_world::home::HomeRoom;
use chat_world::transcript::{sign_transcript, verify_transcript, TranscriptEntry};
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn make_room() -> Room {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn room_verifies_after_activity() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "hello", "normal")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn non_member_is_refused() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let rec = room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "not_a_member"),
        _ => panic!("expected refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn rate_limit_enforced() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.rate_limit_count = 2;
    room.template.rate_limit_window = 5;
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "one", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "two", "normal")?;
    let rec = room.post(&bob, "three", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "rate_limit"),
        _ => panic!("expected rate-limit refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn inter_ai_pace_fires() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.inter_ai_pace = 1;
    let ai_a = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_b = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&ai_a, "communicate", true)?;
    room.join(&ai_b, "communicate", true)?;
    room.post(&ai_a, "first", "normal")?;
    let rec = room.post(&ai_b, "second", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "inter_ai_pace"),
        _ => panic!("expected inter_ai_pace refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn author_can_redact_own() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "oops", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&bob, &h, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    match r {
        Record::Redact(_) => {}
        _ => panic!("expected redact"),
    }
    assert!(room.is_redacted(&h));
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn moderator_can_redact_other() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let moderator = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&moderator, "control", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "spam", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    room.redact(&moderator, &h, "spam", "spam")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn non_moderator_cannot_redact_other() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&alice, &h, "spam", "no")?;
    match r {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "not_allowed"),
        _ => panic!("expected refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn redact_rejects_unknown_rule() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "text", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&bob, &h, "made_up_rule", "no")?;
    match r {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "rule_not_allowed"),
        _ => panic!("expected rule refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn restore_makes_text_visible() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    let before = room.visible_log();
    let view = before.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message") && v["redacted"].as_bool() == Some(true))
        .expect("no redacted message in view");
    assert_eq!(view["text"].as_str(), Some("[removed]"));
    assert!(view["incident"].as_str().is_some());
    assert!(view["rule"].as_str().is_some());
    assert!(view["reason"].as_str().is_some());

    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    let after = room.visible_log();
    let view = after.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message")
              && v["text"].as_str() == Some("words I take back"))
        .expect("message not visible after restore");
    assert!(view["restored_incidents"].is_array());
    let restores = view["restored_incidents"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(restores.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(
        restores[0]["incident"].as_str(),
        Some(&redact_hash[..16])
    );

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn redacted_record_unchanged_after_restore() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    let stored = room.records().iter()
        .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&msg_hash))
        .expect("original message not found");
    match stored {
        Record::Message(m) => assert_eq!(m.text, "words I take back"),
        _ => panic!("original record was not a message"),
    }

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn restore_rejects_non_redact_target() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "hello", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    assert!(room.restore_incident(&dave, &msg_hash, "test").is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn human_can_override_ai() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let ai = Keypair::generate();
    let human = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&ai, "control", true)?;
    room.join(&human, "control", false)?;
    let ai_msg = room.post(&ai, "an observation", "normal")?;
    let h = ai_msg.hash()?;
    room.override_ai(&human, &h, "good observation")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn ai_cannot_override() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let ai1 = Keypair::generate();
    let ai2 = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&ai1, "control", true)?;
    room.join(&ai2, "communicate", true)?;
    let msg = room.post(&ai2, "hi", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    assert!(room.override_ai(&ai1, &h, "no").is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn personal_room_refuses_non_seed() -> Result<()> {
    let user = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let template = RoomTemplate::personal_home("my home");
    let mut room = Room::create(template, room_kp, &user)?;
    let other = Keypair::generate();
    let r = room.join(&other, "communicate", false)?;
    match r {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "personal_room"),
        _ => panic!("expected personal_room refusal"),
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[test]

    let creator = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    template.membership = "human_only".into();
    let mut room = Room::create(template, room_kp, &creator)?;
    let ai = Keypair::generate();
    let r = room.join(&ai, "communicate", true)?;
    match r {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "human_only_room"),
        _ => panic!("expected human_only_room refusal"),
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn chain_integrity_detects_tampering() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    let recs = room.records();
    for i in 1..recs.len() {
        assert_eq!(recs[i].prev(), Some(&recs[i - 1].hash()?));
    }
    Ok(())
}

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let commons_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), room_kp, &alice)?;
    let mut commons = Commons::new(commons_kp);
    commons.register_room(&room, &alice, None)?;
    verify_commons(&commons)
}

#[test]
fn commit_draft_links_home_and_room() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let home_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let bob_room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice.clone(), home_kp, "test home")?;
    let mut bob_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), bob_room_kp, &bob)?;
    bob_room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let draft = home.draft("hello", Some(bob_room.world_id()))?;
    let draft_hash = draft.hash()?;
    let committed = home.commit_draft_to_room(&draft_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    match committed {
        Record::Message(m) => {
            assert!(m.text.contains("home_ref="));
            assert!(m.text.contains(&draft_hash[..16]));
        }
        _ => panic!("expected message"),
    }
    verify_room(home.room())?;
    verify_room(&bob_room)?;
    Ok(())
}
```

src/bin/chat-demo.rs

```rust
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("Chat World — walking the standard end to end");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let carol = Keypair::generate();
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_mod = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_part = Keypair::generate();

    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat(
        "chat-room-v1",
        "how should a room hold disagreement?",
    );
    template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    template.membership = "mixed".into();
    template.inter_ai_pace = 1;

    let mut room = Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice)?;
    println!("Room:     {} (room's own key)", short(room.world_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Creator:  {} (alice — joins at terminal)", short(alice.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Moderator: {} (ai-mod, declared in template)", short(ai_mod.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!();

    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&carol, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&ai_mod, "control", true)?;
    room.join(&ai_part, "communicate", true)?;
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    println!("Joined: bob, carol, dave (human, control),");
    println!("        ai-mod (AI, control), ai-part (AI, communicate)");
    println!();

    room.post(
        &bob,
        "I think a room should make room for disagreement without turning into a fight.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Welcome, Bob. The topic here is 'how should a room hold disagreement?'. Introduce yourself or ask a question.",
        "welcome",
    )?;
    println!("Bob posted; ai-mod welcomed.");

    room.post(
        &ai_part,
        "The key is whether the room treats disagreement as a resource or as a hazard.",
        "response",
    )?;
    println!("ai-part responded.");

    let follow_up = room.post(
        &ai_mod,
        "Building on that — what does 'resource' mean here?",
        "prompt",
    )?;
    match &follow_up {
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod follow-up refused: {}", r.reason),
        Record::Message(_) => println!("ai-mod follow-up accepted (pace may not have fired)"),
        _ => println!("ai-mod follow-up produced unexpected record"),
    }

    room.post(
        &carol,
        "Hazard is when someone feels unsafe; resource is when the disagreement is about ideas.",
        "normal",
    )?;
    println!("carol posted; pacing reset.");

    let short_msg = room.post(&ai_part, "k", "normal")?;
    let short_hash = short_msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&ai_mod, &short_hash, "low_effort", "message too short to be useful")?;
    match &redact {
        Record::Redact(_) => println!("ai-mod redacted ai-part's low-effort message."),
        Record::Refusal(r) => println!("ai-mod redaction refused: {}", r.reason),
        _ => {}
    }

    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;
    let restore = room.restore_incident(
        &dave,
        &redact_hash,
        "I want this message to stand. The AI was too hasty.",
    )?;
    match &restore {
        Record::HumanOverride(o) => println!(
            "dave restored the redaction: action={}, reason={:?}",
            o.action, o.reason
        ),
        _ => {}
    }

    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    let verify_ok = match verify_room(&room) {
        Ok(()) => {
            println!("Verification: room verifies offline (chain, sigs, policy replay).");
            true
        }
        Err(e) => {
            println!("Verification FAILED: {e}");
            false
        }
    };
    println!();

    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Visible log:");
    for entry in room.visible_log() {
        let t = entry["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
        let seq = entry["seq"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
        match t {
            "message" => {
                let author = short(entry["author"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let kind = entry["kind"].as_str().unwrap_or("normal");
                let text = entry["text"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let trunc: String = text.chars().take(44).collect();
                let redacted = entry["redacted"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false);
                if redacted {
                    let incident = entry["incident"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                    let rule = entry["rule"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                    let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                    println!(
                        "  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} [removed: {reason} (rule={rule}, incident={incident})]"
                    );
                } else if let Some(restored) = entry.get("restored_incidents").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
                    if !restored.is_empty() {
                        let incident = restored[0]["incident"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                        println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc} [restored, incident={incident}]");
                    } else {
                        println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc}");
                    }
                } else {
                    println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:10} {trunc}");
                }
            }
            "join" => {
                let subj = short(entry["subject"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let tier = entry["tier"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                let tag = if entry["is_ai"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false) { "AI" } else { "human" };
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] join: {subj} ({tier}, {tag})");
            }
            "redact" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let rule = entry["rule"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let role = entry["actor_role"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let incident = entry["incident"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!(
                    "  [{seq:>3}] redact by {by} (as {role}, rule={rule}): {reason:?} [incident={incident}]"
                );
            }
            "human_override" => {
                let by = short(entry["by"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let action = entry["action"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] human_override ({action}) by {by}: {reason:?}");
            }
            "refused" => {
                let who = short(entry["requester"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 12);
                let reason = entry["reason"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] refused: {who} ({reason})");
            }
            "created" => println!("  [{seq:>3}] created"),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    println!();
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("What this demonstrates:");
    println!("  - The room is its own world with its own key; the");
    println!("    creator joins with their key, not as the room.");
    println!("    A room can be handed on without anyone's own key");
    println!("    changing hands.");
    println!("  - The room is a policy-gated append-only chain.");
    println!("  - The AI moderator signs its own records; a pace rule");
    println!("    stopped an AI-to-AI follow-up.");
    println!("  - Redaction names role, rule, reason; incident id is");
    println!("    the redact record's hash, checkable by anyone.");
    println!("  - The removed message's record stays unchanged; the");
    println!("    presentation shows a note with reason and incident.");
    println!("  - A human moderator (dave) restored the incident;");
    println!("    the message is visible with a restore note.");
    if verify_ok {
        println!("  - The whole room verifies offline.");
    } else {
        println!("  - (Verification failed; see above.)");
    }
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'The Brain – is wider than the Sky –'");
    println!("  'For – put them side by side –'");

    Ok(())
}
```

src/bin/home-demo.rs

```rust
use chat_world::commons::{verify_commons, Commons};
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::home::HomeRoom;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::transcript::{sign_transcript, TranscriptEntry};
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn check_home_ref(rec: &Record, home: &HomeRoom) -> Option<String> {
    let text = match rec {
        Record::Message(m) => &m.text,
        _ => return None,
    };
    let idx = text.find("home_ref=")?;
    let rest = &text[idx + 9..];
    let prefix: String = rest.chars().take(16).collect();
    let found = home
        .room()
        .records()
        .iter()
        .any(|r| r.hash().map(|h| h.starts_with(&prefix)).unwrap_or(false));
    Some(format!("home_ref={prefix}: {}", if found { "found" } else { "MISSING" }))
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("Home Room + Commons — the user's journey outward");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let home_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let bob_room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let commons_kp = Keypair::generate();

    // ---- Alice creates her home ----
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice.clone(), home_kp, "alice's home")?;
    println!("Alice's home:  {}", short(home.room().world_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("  (room's own key; alice is creator)");
    println!("  template:    {}", home.room().template().template_id);
    println!("  personal:    {}", home.room().template().personal);
    println!();

    // ---- Journal ----
    home.journal("First entry. I want to understand how composition works at three levels.")?;
    home.journal("Note: the parent supplies, the child decides.")?;
    println!("Journals: 2 entries");

    // ---- Bob creates a room ----
    let bob_template = RoomTemplate::chat("fractal-worlds", "fractal worlds");
    let mut bob_room = Room::create(bob_template, bob_room_kp, &bob)?;
    println!("Bob's room:    {}", short(bob_room.world_id().as_str(), 32));

    // ---- Commons registers Bob's room ----
    let mut commons = Commons::new(commons_kp);
    commons.register_room(&bob_room, &bob, None)?;
    println!("Commons:       {}", short(commons.world_id.as_str(), 32));
    println!("  registered {} room(s)", commons.records().len());
    println!();

    // ---- Alice drafts a message for Bob's room ----
    let draft = home.draft(
        "I've been thinking about the composition rule. What if a plant could refuse even when the garden gives it water?",
        Some(bob_room.world_id()),
    )?;
    let draft_hash = draft.hash()?;
    println!("Draft:     {}", short(&draft_hash, 20));

    // ---- Alice ports an external conversation ----
    let entries = vec![
        TranscriptEntry {
            speaker: "user".into(),
            ts: chat_world::now(),
            text: "What is composition in fractal worlds?".into(),
        },
        TranscriptEntry {
            speaker: "assistant".into(),
            ts: chat_world::now(),
            text: "The parent delegates the child's law to the child. It enforces only interaction rules.".into(),
        },
    ];
    let transcript = sign_transcript("claude.ai session, 2026-11-15", entries, &alice)?;
    let port = home.port(&transcript, "room", Some(bob_room.world_id()))?;
    let port_hash = port.hash()?;
    println!("Port:      {} (from {})", short(&port_hash, 20), transcript.source_label);

    // ---- Bookmark + presence ----
    home.bookmark(&bob_room, "good conversation, come back")?;
    home.announce_presence(&[bob_room.world_id().clone()], 300)?;
    println!("Bookmark + presence recorded.");
    println!();

    // ---- Alice joins Bob's room ----
    bob_room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    println!("Alice joined Bob's room (tier: communicate).");

    // ---- Alice commits her draft and port ----
    let committed_draft = home.commit_draft_to_room(&draft_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    println!("Committed draft: {}", short(&committed_draft.hash()?, 20));
    let committed_port = home.commit_port_to_room(&port_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    println!("Committed port:  {}", short(&committed_port.hash()?, 20));
    println!();

    // ---- Verify all three ----
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Verification:");
    match verify_room(home.room()) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  home:    verifies (chain, sigs, policy)"),
        Err(e) => println!("  home:    FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    match verify_room(&bob_room) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  bob's:   verifies"),
        Err(e) => println!("  bob's:   FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    match verify_commons(&commons) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  commons: verifies"),
        Err(e) => println!("  commons: FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    println!();

    // ---- Cross-check home_refs ----
    println!("Cross-references (home_ref in Bob's room → home):");
    for (label, rec) in [("draft", &committed_draft), ("port", &committed_port)] {
        match check_home_ref(rec, &home) {
            Some(msg) => println!("  {label}: {msg}"),
            None => println!("  {label}: no home_ref found"),
        }
    }
    println!();

    // ---- Export ----
    let export = home.export()?;
    let count = export["records"].as_array().map(|a| a.len()).unwrap_or(0);
    println!("Home export: {} records, portable JSON", count);
    println!();

    // ---- Visible log of Bob's room ----
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Bob's room — visible log:");
    for entry in bob_room.visible_log() {
        let t = entry["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
        let seq = entry["seq"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
        match t {
            "message" => {
                let author = short(entry["author"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 10);
                let kind = entry["kind"].as_str().unwrap_or("normal");
                let text = entry["text"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                let trunc: String = text.chars().take(48).collect();
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] {author} {kind:8} {trunc}");
            }
            "join" => {
                let subj = short(entry["subject"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"), 10);
                let tier = entry["tier"].as_str().unwrap_or("?");
                println!("  [{seq:>3}] join: {subj} ({tier})");
            }
            "created" => println!("  [{seq:>3}] created"),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    println!();

    println!("What this establishes:");
    println!("  - The room is its own world with its own key; the");
    println!("    creator joins with theirs. A home room's user");
    println!("    persists the room's key (the demo generates fresh).");
    println!("  - The home is a personal room. Only the creator can");
    println!("    be a member. Journals, drafts, ports, bookmarks,");
    println!("    and presence all live in one chain.");
    println!("  - The home is the user's KnowledgeWorld — one");
    println!("    chain, one identity, one export.");
    println!("  - Committing to a room is explicit. The message");
    println!("    carries a home_ref, so the two logs bind.");
    println!("  - The Commons is a directory, not a gatekeeper.");
    println!("  - All three logs verify offline.");
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'I'm Nobody! Who are you?'");
    println!("  'Are you – Nobody – too?'");

    Ok(())
}
```

To check by running: cargo build && cargo test && cargo run --bin chat-demo && cargo run --bin home-demo.

Expected: all 23 tests pass. chat-demo prints Room: <room_kp> and Creator: <alice> as distinct identities; ai-mod's redaction shows rule=low_effort, incident=...; dave's restore is a human_override (restore). home-demo prints Alice's home: <home_kp> distinct from alice; all three logs verify; home_ref cross-references found.

A note to carry forward in the reply: The room_kp in HomeRoom::create is now the caller's responsibility. For the crate this is fine — the demo generates fresh. In production, the app must persist it, or the home's identity changes on every restart. That's a real design decision worth naming when this gets fitted.

Step 3 next: prose on signed heads — what is lost, if anything, when a room signs its log's head every so many records rather than every record. Then the code.


::➡️


Step 3 — signed heads, first in prose, then code.

Is anything lost if a room signs its log's head every so many records, in place of signing each record?

Three real losses, in decreasing importance:

1. Immediate room acceptance proof. Under per-record signing, every record has the room's signature and is fully verifiable on receipt. Under head-signing, a record has only its author's signature on receipt; the room's acceptance is provable once a head covering the record is published. For a busy room with a large batch size, that's minutes of finality latency.
2. Per-record room commitment. A reader can no longer say "the room signed this specific record at this position." They can say "the author signed this record; a head exists that covers position K." Semantically equivalent when both are present, but the shape of the evidence changes.
3. A one-batch equivocation window. An adversarial room has a batch-window during which it could publish a shorter head excluding a record. Anyone holding the record can prove misbehavior (the record's author proof + the shorter head's length < the record's position), but the room gets to try. Same trade-off Bitcoin makes for block confirmations.

What is NOT lost:

· Chain integrity — prev links are intact. Substituting, removing, or reordering breaks the chain and the head's last.
· Author attribution — every record still carries its author's proof.
· Policy replay — verify_records runs identically on the full log.
· Refusal accountability — refusals are records; heads cover them.
· Leave's subject-only signature — unchanged.
· Personal-room closure — unchanged.

What is gained:

1. The room's signing key can be offline between batches. A huge security win for a long-lived room.
2. The room's signing cost drops linearly — N records → N/batch heads.
3. Federation becomes first-class. A head alone is a binding commitment; a peer can verify without downloading the log.
4. Batch verification — verify the head, verify each record's author proof, done.
5. Honest migration — both modes coexist in the protocol.

The right frame. The room's signature moves from the record to the head. The record's own author proof becomes the only per-record signature. Chain integrity is self-evident. The room's role becomes committing to the head, not vouching for each record.

For a chat room, this is the right trade. Chat is fast, batches are short, and the finality-latency cost is small. The key-offline win is large, because chat rooms are long-lived and would otherwise need the room's key live forever.

What stands in for a room's tick. The project's head format has a tick field. Rooms have no ticks. I propose: tick = length. A room's "time" is its accumulated record count — monotonic, deterministic, needs no clock. A head signed at length 42 has tick 42. If a wall clock ever matters, it belongs in a separate record, not in the head.

---

The code. Eight files: template.rs, head.rs (new), lib.rs, room.rs, verify.rs, home.rs, tests/integration.rs, bin/head-demo.rs (new). chat-demo.rs and home-demo.rs are unchanged — they stay in per-record mode.

```rust
use crate::did::DidKey;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ModeratorSpec {
    pub moderator_id: DidKey,
    pub moderator_type: String,
    pub capabilities: Vec<String>,
    pub can_be_overridden: bool,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub knowledge_world_id: Option<DidKey>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub meta_room: bool,
}

fn is_false(b: &bool) -> bool { !*b }

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RoomTemplate {
    pub template_id: String,
    pub topic: String,
    pub max_msg_len: u64,
    pub rate_limit_count: u64,
    pub rate_limit_window: u64,
    pub join_policy: String,
    pub moderation: String,
    pub membership: String,

    pub mode: String,
    pub personal: bool,
    pub redaction_rules: Vec<String>,
    /// Signing mode. `1` (default): the room signs every record
    /// (dual-sign). `N > 1`: the room signs a head every N
    /// records appended after the seed; individual records carry
    /// only their author's proof.
    pub head_interval: u64,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]

    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub moderator: Option<ModeratorSpec>,
}

        RoomTemplate {
            template_id: template_id.into(),
            topic: topic.into(),
            max_msg_len: 4000,
            rate_limit_count: 10,
            rate_limit_window: 20,
            join_policy: "open".into(),
            moderation: "moderator".into(),
            membership: "mixed".into(),
            inter_ai_pace: 0,
            mode: "async".into(),
            personal: false,
            redaction_rules: vec![
                "author_request".into(),
                "spam".into(),
                "low_effort".into(),
                "off_topic".into(),
                "harassment".into(),
                "private_info".into(),
            ],
            head_interval: 1,
            welcome: None,
            moderator: None,
        }
    }

            template_id: "home-v1".into(),
            topic: topic.into(),
            max_msg_len: 8000,
            rate_limit_count: 100,
            rate_limit_window: 100,
            join_policy: "invite".into(),
            moderation: "author_only".into(),
            membership: "human_only".into(),
            inter_ai_pace: 0,
            mode: "async".into(),
            personal: true,
            redaction_rules: vec![
                "author_request".into(),
                "private_info".into(),
            ],
            head_interval: 1,
            welcome: None,
            moderator: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> {
        crate::hash::hash_of(self)
    }
```
