src/head.rs (new)

```rust
//! Signed heads.
//!
//! A head is a small, signed commitment to a log's prefix: how
//! many entries it holds, what the last one hashes to, and when
//! (in the room's sense of "when"). It is a *published artifact*,
//! not a record in the log. A room in `head_interval > 1` mode
//! emits a head every N appended records; peers can verify a
//! head alone and know the log's prefix is fixed.

use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::proof::{Proof, ProofOrArray};
use crate::room::Room;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const HEAD_FORMAT_V1: &str = "open-worlds/log-head/v1";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WorldHead {
    pub format: String,
    pub world: DidKey,
    pub length: u64,
    pub last: String,
    pub tick: u64,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

impl WorldHead {
    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> {
        hash::hash_of(self)
    }
}

impl Room {
    /// Emit a head for the room's current state. Does NOT append
    /// to the room's log; the head is a published artifact.
    pub fn emit_head(&self) -> Result<WorldHead> {
        let last_rec = self
            .records()
            .last()
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Policy("cannot emit head for empty room".into()))?;
        let last_hash = last_rec.hash()?;
        let length = self.records().len() as u64;
        let mut head = WorldHead {
            format: HEAD_FORMAT_V1.into(),
            world: self.world_id().clone(),
            length,
            last: last_hash,
            tick: length,
            proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder(),
        };
        let body = hash::body_for_signing(&head)?;
        let sig = self.keypair().sign(&body);
        head.proof = ProofOrArray::single(Proof::create(self.keypair(), &sig));
        Ok(head)
    }

    /// Verify a head against the room it claims to be about.
    /// The room must be at least as long as the head; the head's
    /// prefix must be intact and its signature valid.
    pub fn verify_head(&self, head: &WorldHead) -> Result<()> {
        if head.format != HEAD_FORMAT_V1 {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head: bad format {:?}",
                head.format
            )));
        }
        if head.world != *self.world_id() {
            return Err(Error::Verification("head: world mismatch".into()));
        }
        if head.length as usize > self.records().len() {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head: length {} exceeds log length {}",
                head.length,
                self.records().len()
            )));
        }
        if head.length == 0 {
            return Err(Error::Verification("head: length 0".into()));
        }
        if head.length != head.tick {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head: tick {} != length {}",
                head.tick, head.length
            )));
        }
        let prefix = &self.records()[..head.length as usize];
        let last_hash = prefix[prefix.len() - 1].hash()?;
        if last_hash != head.last {
            return Err(Error::Verification("head: last mismatch".into()));
        }
        let body = hash::body_for_signing(head)?;
        let proof = head
            .proof
            .find_by_method(self.world_id())
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification("head: missing room proof".into()))?;
        proof.verify(&body)
    }
}
```

src/lib.rs

```rust
pub mod error;
pub mod hash;
pub mod did;
pub mod proof;
pub mod template;
pub mod record;
pub mod room;
pub mod verify;
pub mod transcript;
pub mod home;
pub mod commons;
pub mod head;

pub use did::{DidKey, Keypair};
pub use error::{Error, Result};
pub use head::WorldHead;
pub use proof::{Proof, ProofOrArray, ProofPurpose};
pub use room::{Member, Room};
pub use template::{ModeratorSpec, RoomTemplate};

pub fn now() -> String {
    chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339_opts(chrono::SecondsFormat::Millis, true)
}

pub(crate) fn sign_record<T, F>(
    mut rec: T,
    signer: &Keypair,
    wrap: fn(T) -> record::Record,
    set_proof: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    T: serde::Serialize + Clone,
    F: FnOnce(&mut T, ProofOrArray),
{
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&wrap(rec.clone()))?;
    let sig = signer.sign(&body);
    let proof = ProofOrArray::Single(Proof::create(signer, &sig));
    set_proof(&mut rec, proof);
    Ok(rec)
}

pub(crate) fn sign_record_dual<T, F>(
    mut rec: T,
    subject: &Keypair,
    room: &Keypair,
    wrap: fn(T) -> record::Record,
    set_proof: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    T: serde::Serialize + Clone,
    F: FnOnce(&mut T, ProofOrArray),
{
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&wrap(rec.clone()))?;
    let subj_sig = subject.sign(&body);
    let room_sig = room.sign(&body);
    let subj_proof = Proof::create(subject, &subj_sig);
    let room_proof = Proof::create(room, &room_sig);
    set_proof(&mut rec, ProofOrArray::array(vec![subj_proof, room_proof]));
    Ok(rec)
}
```

src/room.rs

```rust
use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::head::WorldHead;
use crate::record::*;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

fn short_hash(h: &str) -> String {
    h.chars().take(16).collect()
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Member {
    pub tier: String,
    pub is_ai: bool,
}

pub struct Room {
    pub world_id: DidKey,
    pub template: RoomTemplate,
    keypair: Keypair,
    records: Vec<Record>,
    members: HashMap<DidKey, Member>,
    heads: Vec<WorldHead>,
}

impl Room {
    /// Create a room. The room has its own key; the creator joins
    /// as the first (terminal-tier) member. Every world is a key of
    /// its own, so a room can be handed on without anyone's personal
    /// key changing hands.
    pub fn create(
        template: RoomTemplate,
        room_kp: Keypair,
        creator_kp: &Keypair,
    ) -> Result<Self> {
        let world_id = room_kp.did();
        let creator_id = creator_kp.did();
        let mut members = HashMap::new();
        members.insert(
            creator_id.clone(),
            Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false },
        );
        let mut room = Room {
            world_id: world_id.clone(),
            template: template.clone(),
            keypair: room_kp,
            records: Vec::new(),
            members,
            heads: Vec::new(),
        };
        let rec = RoomCreated::new(world_id, template, creator_id);
        let signed = crate::sign_record_dual(
            rec, creator_kp, &room.keypair, Record::RoomCreated, |r, p| r.proof = p,
        )?;
        room.records.push(Record::RoomCreated(signed));
        Ok(room)
    }

    pub fn records(&self) -> &[Record] { &self.records }
    pub fn heads(&self) -> &[WorldHead] { &self.heads }
    pub fn last_hash(&self) -> Option<String> { self.records.last().and_then(|r| r.hash().ok()) }
    pub fn world_id(&self) -> &DidKey { &self.world_id }
    pub fn template(&self) -> &RoomTemplate { &self.template }
    pub fn members(&self) -> &HashMap<DidKey, Member> { &self.members }
    pub fn keypair(&self) -> &Keypair { &self.keypair }
    pub fn head_interval(&self) -> u64 { self.template.head_interval }

    pub fn is_redacted(&self, message_hash: &str) -> bool {
        let mut redact_hashes: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut resolved: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
        for rec in &self.records {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) if r.target_hash == message_hash => {
                    if let Ok(h) = rec.hash() { redact_hashes.push(h); }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    resolved.insert(o.overrides_hash.clone());
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        redact_hashes.iter().any(|h| !resolved.contains(h))
    }

    fn next_seq(&self) -> u64 { self.records.len() as u64 }

    fn should_emit_head(&self) -> bool {
        let n = self.template.head_interval;
        if n <= 1 { return false; }
        let appends = self.records.len().saturating_sub(1) as u64;
        appends > 0 && appends % n == 0
    }

    pub(crate) fn push(&mut self, rec: Record) -> Result<()> {
        self.records.push(rec);
        if self.should_emit_head() {
            let head = self.emit_head()?;
            self.heads.push(head);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn refuse(&mut self, requester: DidKey, request_hash: String, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Refusal::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            request_hash, requester, reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.keypair, Record::Refusal, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let out = Record::Refusal(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn rate_ok(&self, author: &DidKey) -> bool {
        let window = self.template.rate_limit_window as usize;
        let cap = self.template.rate_limit_count as usize;
        let recent = self.records.iter().rev().take(window)
            .filter(|r| matches!(r, Record::Message(m) if &m.author_id == author))
            .count();
        recent < cap
    }

    fn inter_ai_pace_ok(&self, is_ai: bool) -> bool {
        if !is_ai || self.template.inter_ai_pace == 0 {
            return true;
        }
        let mut last_ai_idx: Option<usize> = None;
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate().rev() {
            if let Record::Message(m) = rec {
                let ai = self.members.get(&m.author_id).map(|x| x.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                if ai {
                    last_ai_idx = Some(i);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        match last_ai_idx {
            None => true,
            Some(idx) => {
                let since = (self.records.len() - idx - 1) as u64;
                since >= self.template.inter_ai_pace
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn join(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair, tier: &str, is_ai: bool) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "already_member");
        }
        if tier_rank(tier).is_none() {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "unknown_tier");
        }
        if self.template.personal {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "personal_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "human_only" && is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "human_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.membership == "ai_only" && !is_ai {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "ai_only_room");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "invite" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "invite_required");
        }
        if self.template.join_policy == "approval" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "approval_required");
        }
        let rec = Join::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), tier.into(), is_ai,
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Join, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Join, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        self.members.insert(subject, Member { tier: tier.into(), is_ai });
        let out = Record::Join(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn leave(&mut self, subject_kp: &Keypair) -> Result<Option<Record>> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        if !self.members.contains_key(&subject) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let rec = Leave::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(), subject.clone(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Leave, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        self.members.remove(&subject);
        let out = Record::Leave(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(Some(out))
    }

    pub fn post(&mut self, author_kp: &Keypair, text: &str, kind: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let author = author_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&author).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "insufficient_tier");
        }
        if text.len() as u64 > self.template.max_msg_len {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "message_too_long");
        }
        if !self.rate_ok(&author) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "rate_limit");
        }
        if !self.inter_ai_pace_ok(m.is_ai) {
            return self.refuse(author, "".into(), "inter_ai_pace");
        }
        let rec = Message::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            author.clone(), text.into(), kind.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, author_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Message, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, author_kp, Record::Message, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::Message(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn redact(
        &mut self,
        subject_kp: &Keypair,
        target_hash: &str,
        rule: &str,
        reason: &str,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let subject = subject_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&subject).cloned() else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_a_member");
        };
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(target_hash));
        let target_msg = match target {
            Some(Record::Message(msg)) => msg,
            _ => return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "target_not_found"),
        };
        if self.template.moderation == "none" {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "moderation_disabled");
        }
        let is_author = target_msg.author_id == subject;
        let is_mod = self.template.moderation == "moderator"
            && tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
        let actor_role = if is_author {
            "author"
        } else if is_mod {
            "moderator"
        } else {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "not_allowed");
        };
        if !self.template.redaction_rules.iter().any(|r| r == rule) {
            return self.refuse(subject, "".into(), "rule_not_allowed");
        }
        let rec = Redact::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            subject.clone(), target_hash.into(),
            actor_role.into(), rule.into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, subject_kp, &self.keypair, Record::Redact, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, subject_kp, Record::Redact, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::Redact(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn override_ai(&mut self, overrider_kp: &Keypair, overrides_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let overrider = overrider_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&overrider).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("overrider is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can override".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(overrides_hash));
        let Some(target_rec) = target else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target not found".into()));
        };
        let Some(author) = target_rec.author_id() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("target has no author".into()));
        };
        let is_ai = self.members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
        if !is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("can only override AI records".into()));
        }
        if let Some(spec) = &self.template.moderator {
            if &spec.moderator_id == author && !spec.can_be_overridden {
                return Err(Error::Policy("this AI cannot be overridden".into()));
            }
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            overrides_hash.into(), overrider.clone(), "none".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, overrider_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, overrider_kp, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn restore_incident(&mut self, restorer_kp: &Keypair, redact_hash: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let restorer = restorer_kp.did();
        let Some(m) = self.members.get(&restorer).cloned() else {
            return Err(Error::Policy("restorer is not a member".into()));
        };
        if m.is_ai {
            return Err(Error::Policy("only humans can restore".into()));
        }
        if tier_rank(&m.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("insufficient tier".into()));
        }
        let target = self.records.iter()
            .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(redact_hash));
        match target {
            Some(Record::Redact(_)) => {}
            _ => return Err(Error::Policy("restore target must be a redact record".into())),
        }
        let rec = HumanOverride::new(
            self.world_id.clone(), self.next_seq(), self.last_hash(),
            redact_hash.into(), restorer.clone(), "restore".into(), reason.into(),
        );
        let signed = if self.template.head_interval <= 1 {
            crate::sign_record_dual(rec, restorer_kp, &self.keypair, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        } else {
            crate::sign_record(rec, restorer_kp, Record::HumanOverride, |r, p| r.proof = p)?
        };
        let out = Record::HumanOverride(signed);
        self.push(out.clone())?;
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn visible_log(&self) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
        use serde_json::json;

        let hashes: Vec<String> = self.records.iter()
            .map(|r| r.hash().unwrap_or_default())
            .collect();
        let mut hash_to_idx: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();
        for (i, h) in hashes.iter().enumerate() {
            hash_to_idx.insert(h.clone(), i);
        }

        let mut redactions_by_target: HashMap<usize, Vec<usize>> = HashMap::new();
        let mut resolved_redacts: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    if let Some(&target_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&r.target_hash) {
                        redactions_by_target.entry(target_idx).or_default().push(i);
                    }
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) if o.action == "restore" => {
                    if let Some(&redact_idx) = hash_to_idx.get(&o.overrides_hash) {
                        resolved_redacts.insert(redact_idx);
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for (i, rec) in self.records.iter().enumerate() {
            match rec {
                Record::Message(m) => {
                    let mut still_redacted: Option<usize> = None;
                    let mut restored: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
                    if let Some(redact_idxs) = redactions_by_target.get(&i) {
                        for &ri in redact_idxs {
                            if resolved_redacts.contains(&ri) {
                                restored.push(ri);
                            } else if still_redacted.is_none() {
                                still_redacted = Some(ri);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if let Some(ri) = still_redacted {
                        if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                            out.push(json!({
                                "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                                "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                                "text": "[removed]",
                                "redacted": true,
                                "reason": r.reason,
                                "rule": r.rule,
                                "incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]),
                                "ts": m.ts,
                            }));
                        }
                    } else {
                        let mut obj = json!({
                            "seq": m.seq, "type": "message", "kind": m.kind,
                            "author": m.author_id.as_str(),
                            "text": m.text,
                            "redacted": false,
                            "ts": m.ts,
                        });
                        if !restored.is_empty() {
                            let notes: Vec<serde_json::Value> = restored.iter().map(|&ri| {
                                if let Record::Redact(r) = &self.records[ri] {
                                    json!({
                                        "incident": short_hash(&hashes[ri]),
                                        "reason": r.reason,
                                        "rule": r.rule,
                                    })
                                } else {
                                    json!({})
                                }
                            }).collect();
                            obj["restored_incidents"] = json!(notes);
                        }
                        out.push(obj);
                    }
                }
                Record::Redact(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({
                        "seq": r.seq, "type": "redact",
                        "by": r.subject_id.as_str(),
                        "reason": r.reason,
                        "target": r.target_hash,
                        "rule": r.rule,
                        "actor_role": r.actor_role,
                        "incident": short_hash(&hashes[i]),
                    }));
                }
                Record::Join(j) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": j.seq, "type": "join", "subject": j.subject_id.as_str(), "tier": j.tier, "is_ai": j.is_ai}));
                }
                Record::Leave(l) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": l.seq, "type": "leave", "subject": l.subject_id.as_str()}));
                }
                Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": o.seq, "type": "human_override", "by": o.overridden_by.as_str(), "overrides": o.overrides_hash, "action": o.action, "reason": o.reason}));
                }
                Record::Refusal(r) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": r.seq, "type": "refused", "requester": r.requester_id.as_str(), "reason": r.reason}));
                }
                Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                    out.push(json!({"seq": 0, "type": "created"}));
                }
                Record::PortLanding(_) | Record::Bookmark(_) | Record::Presence(_) => {}
            }
        }
        out
    }
}
```

src/verify.rs

```rust
use crate::did::DidKey;
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::record::*;
use crate::room::{Member, Room};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

/// Full offline verification: chain, signatures, heads, policy replay.
pub fn verify_room(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let world_id = room.world_id();
    let (creator_id, per_record_mode) = match room.records().first() {
        Some(Record::RoomCreated(rc)) => {
            (rc.creator_id.clone(), rc.template.head_interval <= 1)
        }
        _ => return Err(Error::Chain("log must start with room_created".into())),
    };

    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    let mut members: HashMap<DidKey, Member> = HashMap::new();
    let mut redacted: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();

    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {
                if i != 0 {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: room_created not at start")));
                }
            }
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }

        let body = hash::body_for_signing(rec)?;
        let proofs = rec.proof();

        // Room proof requirement:
        //   RoomCreated:   always
        //   Refusal:       always (the room's own act)
        //   Leave:         never (subject-only; leaving can't be refused)
        //   Everything else: only in per-record mode
        let requires_room_proof = match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => true,
            Record::Refusal(_) => true,
            Record::Leave(_) => false,
            _ => per_record_mode,
        };
        if requires_room_proof {
            let rp = proofs.find_by_method(world_id).ok_or_else(|| {
                Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing room proof"))
            })?;
            rp.verify(&body)?;
        }

        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(r) => {
                let creator_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.creator_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing creator proof")))?;
                creator_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(r.creator_id.clone(), Member { tier: "terminal".into(), is_ai: false });
            }
            Record::Message(m) => {
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&m.author_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author not a member")));
                };
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("communicate").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: author tier insufficient")));
                }
                let author_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&m.author_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing author proof")))?;
                author_proof.verify(&body)?;
            }
            Record::Join(j) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&j.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.insert(j.subject_id.clone(), Member { tier: j.tier.clone(), is_ai: j.is_ai });
            }
            Record::Leave(l) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&l.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                members.remove(&l.subject_id);
            }
            Record::Redact(r) => {
                let subject_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&r.subject_id)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing subject proof")))?;
                subject_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&r.subject_id) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redactor not a member")));
                };
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&r.target_hash));
                let target_msg = match target {
                    Some(Record::Message(m)) => m,
                    _ => return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: redact target not a message"))),
                };
                if room.template().moderation == "none" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: moderation disabled")));
                }
                let is_author = target_msg.author_id == r.subject_id;
                let is_mod = room.template().moderation == "moderator"
                    && tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) >= tier_rank("control").unwrap();
                let role_ok = match r.actor_role.as_str() {
                    "author" => is_author,
                    "moderator" => is_mod,
                    _ => false,
                };
                if !role_ok {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: actor_role {:?} not permitted (is_author={is_author}, is_mod={is_mod})",
                        r.actor_role
                    )));
                }
                if !room.template().redaction_rules.iter().any(|x| x == &r.rule) {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: rule {:?} not in template's redaction_rules",
                        r.rule
                    )));
                }
                redacted.insert(r.target_hash.clone());
            }
            Record::HumanOverride(o) => {
                let overrider_proof = proofs.find_by_method(&o.overridden_by)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: missing overrider proof")))?;
                overrider_proof.verify(&body)?;
                let Some(mem) = members.get(&o.overridden_by) else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider not a member")));
                };
                if mem.is_ai {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: only humans can override")));
                }
                if tier_rank(&mem.tier).unwrap_or(0) < tier_rank("control").unwrap() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: overrider tier insufficient")));
                }
                let target = room.records().iter()
                    .find(|x| x.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&o.overrides_hash));
                let Some(target) = target else {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override target not found")));
                };
                match o.action.as_str() {
                    "none" => {
                        let Some(author) = target.author_id() else {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target has no author")));
                        };
                        let target_is_ai = members.get(author).map(|m| m.is_ai).unwrap_or(false);
                        if !target_is_ai {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: override 'none' target must be AI-authored")));
                        }
                    }
                    "restore" => {
                        if !matches!(target, Record::Redact(_)) {
                            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: restore target must be a redact record")));
                        }
                    }
                    other => {
                        return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown override action {other:?}")));
                    }
                }
            }
            Record::Refusal(_) => {}
            Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: port_landing only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                crate::transcript::verify_transcript(&p.transcript)
                    .map_err(|e| Error::Verification(format!("record {i}: transcript: {e}")))?;
                if p.intent != "journal" && p.intent != "room" {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: unknown port intent")));
                }
                if p.intent == "room" && p.target_room.is_none() {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!("record {i}: intent 'room' without target_room")));
                }
            }
            Record::Bookmark(_) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: bookmark only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
            }
            Record::Presence(p) => {
                if !room.template().personal {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: presence only valid in personal rooms"
                    )));
                }
                if p.subject_id != creator_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                        "record {i}: presence subject must be the creator"
                    )));
                }
            }
        }

        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }

    // Verify heads
    let mut last_length: u64 = 0;
    for (i, head) in room.heads().iter().enumerate() {
        room.verify_head(head).map_err(|e| {
            Error::Verification(format!("head {i}: {e}"))
        })?;
        if head.length <= last_length {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head {i}: length {} not greater than previous {}",
                head.length, last_length
            )));
        }
        last_length = head.length;
    }

    // Per-record mode must not carry heads.
    if per_record_mode && !room.heads().is_empty() {
        return Err(Error::Verification(
            "per-record mode should not emit heads".into(),
        ));
    }

    // In head mode, if the log is long enough that a head should
    // have been emitted, at least one must be present.
    if !per_record_mode {
        let n = room.template().head_interval;
        let appends = room.records().len().saturating_sub(1) as u64;
        let expected_heads = if n > 1 { appends / n } else { 0 };
        if expected_heads > 0 && room.heads().len() as u64 != expected_heads {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "head count: expected {} for {} appends at interval {}, got {}",
                expected_heads, appends, n, room.heads().len()
            )));
        }
    }

    // Personal room closure (unchanged).
    if room.template().personal {
        for rec in room.records() {
            if let Record::Join(j) = rec {
                if j.subject_id != creator_id {
                    return Err(Error::Policy(
                        "personal room: non-creator joined".into(),
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

pub fn verify_chain(room: &Room) -> Result<()> {
    let mut prev: Option<String> = None;
    for (i, rec) in room.records().iter().enumerate() {
        match rec {
            Record::RoomCreated(_) if i != 0 => {
                return Err(Error::Chain("room_created not at start".into()));
            }
            Record::RoomCreated(_) => {}
            _ => {
                if rec.prev() != prev.as_ref() {
                    return Err(Error::Chain(format!("record {i}: prev mismatch")));
                }
            }
        }
        prev = Some(rec.hash()?);
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

src/home.rs

```rust
use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::record::*;
use crate::room::Room;
use crate::template::RoomTemplate;
use crate::transcript::{verify_transcript, Transcript};

pub struct HomeRoom {
    room: Room,
    user_kp: Keypair,
}

impl HomeRoom {
    /// Create a home. The user is the creator and the only member;
    /// the room has its own key (`room_kp`), which an app should
    /// persist so the home's identity survives restarts.
    pub fn create(user_kp: Keypair, room_kp: Keypair, display_name: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let template = RoomTemplate::personal_home(display_name);
        let room = Room::create(template, room_kp, &user_kp)?;
        Ok(HomeRoom { room, user_kp })
    }

    pub fn room(&self) -> &Room { &self.room }
    pub fn user_id(&self) -> DidKey { self.user_kp.did() }

    pub fn journal(&mut self, text: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        self.room.post(&self.user_kp, text, "journal")
    }

    pub fn draft(&mut self, text: &str, target_room: Option<&DidKey>) -> Result<Record> {
        let text = match target_room {
            Some(t) => format!("[draft→{}] {}", t.as_str(), text),
            None => format!("[draft] {}", text),
        };
        self.room.post(&self.user_kp, &text, "draft")
    }

    pub fn port(
        &mut self,
        transcript: &Transcript,
        intent: &str,
        target_room: Option<&DidKey>,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        verify_transcript(transcript)?;
        if intent != "journal" && intent != "room" {
            return Err(Error::Policy(format!("unknown intent {intent}")));
        }
        if intent == "room" && target_room.is_none() {
            return Err(Error::Policy("intent 'room' requires target_room".into()));
        }
        let rec = PortLanding::new(
            self.room.world_id().clone(),
            self.room.records().len() as u64,
            self.room.last_hash(),
            transcript.clone(),
            intent.into(),
            target_room.cloned(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.user_kp, Record::PortLanding, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let record = Record::PortLanding(signed);
        self.room.push(record.clone())?;
        Ok(record)
    }

    pub fn bookmark(&mut self, room: &Room, note: &str) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Bookmark::new(
            self.room.world_id().clone(),
            self.room.records().len() as u64,
            self.room.last_hash(),
            room.world_id().clone(),
            note.into(),
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.user_kp, Record::Bookmark, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let record = Record::Bookmark(signed);
        self.room.push(record.clone())?;
        Ok(record)
    }

    pub fn announce_presence(&mut self, rooms: &[DidKey], ttl: u64) -> Result<Record> {
        let rec = Presence::new(
            self.room.world_id().clone(),
            self.room.records().len() as u64,
            self.room.last_hash(),
            self.user_kp.did(),
            rooms.to_vec(),
            ttl,
        );
        let signed = crate::sign_record(rec, &self.user_kp, Record::Presence, |r, p| r.proof = p)?;
        let record = Record::Presence(signed);
        self.room.push(record.clone())?;
        Ok(record)
    }

    pub fn commit_draft_to_room(
        &self,
        draft_hash: &str,
        room: &mut Room,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let draft_text: String = {
            let draft = self
                .room
                .records()
                .iter()
                .find(|r| {
                    r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(draft_hash)
                        && matches!(r, Record::Message(m) if m.kind == "draft")
                })
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Policy("draft not found".into()))?;
            match draft {
                Record::Message(m) => m.text.clone(),
                _ => unreachable!(),
            }
        };
        let prefix = format!("[home_ref={}] ", &draft_hash[..16.min(draft_hash.len())]);
        let full_text = format!("{prefix}{draft_text}");
        room.post(&self.user_kp, &full_text, "normal")
    }

    pub fn commit_port_to_room(
        &self,
        port_hash: &str,
        room: &mut Room,
    ) -> Result<Record> {
        let (label, entries_json, target_ok): (String, String, Option<DidKey>) = {
            let port = self
                .room
                .records()
                .iter()
                .find(|r| {
                    r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(port_hash)
                        && matches!(r, Record::PortLanding(_))
                })
                .ok_or_else(|| Error::Policy("port not found".into()))?;
            match port {
                Record::PortLanding(p) => {
                    let entries_json = serde_json::to_string(&p.transcript.entries)
                        .map_err(|e| Error::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                    (p.transcript.source_label.clone(), entries_json, p.target_room.clone())
                }
                _ => unreachable!(),
            }
        };
        if let Some(target) = &target_ok {
            if target != room.world_id() {
                return Err(Error::Policy(
                    "port target_room does not match this room".into(),
                ));
            }
        }
        let prefix = format!(
            "[home_ref={}] [ported from {}] ",
            &port_hash[..16.min(port_hash.len())],
            label
        );
        let full_text = format!("{prefix}{entries_json}");
        room.post(&self.user_kp, &full_text, "port")
    }

    pub fn export(&self) -> Result<serde_json::Value> {
        let mut records = Vec::new();
        for rec in self.room.records() {
            let body = serde_json::to_value(rec)
                .map_err(|e| Error::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
            records.push(serde_json::json!({
                "hash": rec.hash()?,
                "body": body,
            }));
        }
        Ok(serde_json::json!({
            "world_id": self.room.world_id().as_str(),
            "template": self.room.template(),
            "records": records,
        }))
    }
}
```

```rust
use chat_world::commons::{verify_commons, Commons};
use chat_world::home::HomeRoom;
use chat_world::transcript::{sign_transcript, verify_transcript, TranscriptEntry};
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::record::Record;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn make_room() -> Room {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice).unwrap()
}

fn make_head_mode_room(interval: u64) -> Room {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test");
    template.head_interval = interval;
    Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn room_verifies_after_activity() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "hello", "normal")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn non_member_is_refused() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let rec = room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "not_a_member"),
        _ => panic!("expected refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn rate_limit_enforced() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.rate_limit_count = 2;
    room.template.rate_limit_window = 5;
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "one", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "two", "normal")?;
    let rec = room.post(&bob, "three", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "rate_limit"),
        _ => panic!("expected rate-limit refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn inter_ai_pace_fires() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.inter_ai_pace = 1;
    let ai_a = Keypair::generate();
    let ai_b = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&ai_a, "communicate", true)?;
    room.join(&ai_b, "communicate", true)?;
    room.post(&ai_a, "first", "normal")?;
    let rec = room.post(&ai_b, "second", "normal")?;
    match rec {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "inter_ai_pace"),
        _ => panic!("expected inter_ai_pace refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn author_can_redact_own() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "oops", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&bob, &h, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    match r {
        Record::Redact(_) => {}
        _ => panic!("expected redact"),
    }
    assert!(room.is_redacted(&h));
    verify_room(&room)
}

    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let moderator = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&moderator, "control", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "spam", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    room.redact(&moderator, &h, "spam", "spam")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn non_moderator_cannot_redact_other() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    room.template.moderation = "moderator".into();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    let h = msg.hash()?;
    let r = room.redact(&alice, &h, "spam", "no")?;
    match r {
        Record::Refusal(r) => assert_eq!(r.reason, "not_allowed"),
        _ => panic!("expected refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "text", "normal")?;

        _ => panic!("expected rule refusal"),
    }
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn restore_makes_text_visible() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    let before = room.visible_log();
    let view = before.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message") && v["redacted"].as_bool() == Some(true))
        .expect("no redacted message in view");
    assert_eq!(view["text"].as_str(), Some("[removed]"));

    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    let after = room.visible_log();
    let view = after.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message")
              && v["text"].as_str() == Some("words I take back"))
        .expect("message not visible after restore");
    assert!(view["restored_incidents"].is_array());
    assert_eq!(
        view["restored_incidents"][0]["incident"].as_str(),
        Some(&redact_hash[..16])
    );

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn redacted_record_unchanged_after_restore() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    let stored = room.records().iter()
        .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&msg_hash))
        .expect("original message not found");
    match stored {
        Record::Message(m) => assert_eq!(m.text, "words I take back"),
        _ => panic!("original record was not a message"),
    }

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn restore_rejects_non_redact_target() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "hello", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    assert!(room.restore_incident(&dave, &msg_hash, "test").is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn commit_draft_links_home_and_room() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let home_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let bob_room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice.clone(), home_kp, "test home")?;
    let mut bob_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), bob_room_kp, &bob)?;
    bob_room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let draft = home.draft("hello", Some(bob_room.world_id()))?;
    let draft_hash = draft.hash()?;
    let committed = home.commit_draft_to_room(&draft_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    match committed {
        Record::Message(m) => {
            assert!(m.text.contains("home_ref="));
            assert!(m.text.contains(&draft_hash[..16]));
        }
        _ => panic!("expected message"),
    }
    verify_room(home.room())?;
    verify_room(&bob_room)?;
    Ok(())
}

// ---- Step 3: signed heads ----

#[test]
fn head_mode_emits_heads_at_interval() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(3);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;    // records.len()=2, appends=1
    room.post(&bob, "one", "normal")?;          // len=3, appends=2
    room.post(&bob, "two", "normal")?;          // len=4, appends=3 -> head
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(room.heads()[0].length, 4);

    room.post(&bob, "three", "normal")?;        // len=5
    room.post(&bob, "four", "normal")?;         // len=6
    room.post(&bob, "five", "normal")?;         // len=7, appends=6 -> head
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(room.heads()[1].length, 7);
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_mode_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "b", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "c", "normal")?;
    // 3 appends / 2 -> 1 head at length 3 (after appends=2 -> len=3)
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn head_mode_author_only_records() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(5);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    // In head mode, Message and Join have only author proofs
    // (no room proof). RoomCreated does have a room proof.
    let mut room_proof_on_message = false;
    let mut room_proof_on_join = false;
    for rec in room.records() {
        let has_room = rec.proof().find_by_method(room.world_id()).is_some();
        match rec {
            Record::Message(_) if has_room => room_proof_on_message = true,
            Record::Join(_) if has_room => room_proof_on_join = true,
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    assert!(!room_proof_on_message, "Message should not have room proof in head mode");
    assert!(!room_proof_on_join, "Join should not have room proof in head mode");
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn head_verify_against_prefix() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    // appends=2, head at length=3
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 1);
    let head = room.heads()[0].clone();
    room.verify_head(&head)?;
    // Emit a later head; the earlier head still verifies
    room.post(&bob, "b", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "c", "normal")?;
    // appends=4, head at length=5
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 2);
    let head0 = room.heads()[0].clone();
    room.verify_head(&head0)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_rejects_tampered_last() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    let mut head = room.heads()[0].clone();
    head.last = "00".repeat(32);
    assert!(room.verify_head(&head).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_rejects_wrong_tick() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    let mut head = room.heads()[0].clone();
    head.tick = head.length + 1;
    assert!(room.verify_head(&head).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn per_record_mode_rejects_heads() -> Result<()> {
    let room = make_room();
    // Per-record mode; heads should be empty and verify_room
    // rejects if any heads are present. Sanity: heads empty.
    assert!(room.heads().is_empty());
    verify_room(&room)
}
```

src/bin/head-demo.rs (new)

```rust
use chat_world::error::Result;
use chat_world::verify::verify_room;
use chat_world::{Keypair, Room, RoomTemplate};

fn short(s: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    let s = s.strip_prefix("did:key:").unwrap_or(s);
    s.chars().take(n).collect()
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!("Signed Heads — batched commitment, offline key");
    println!("{}", "=".repeat(74));
    println!();

    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();

    // A room in head mode: the room signs a head every 3 appends.
    let mut template = RoomTemplate::chat("head-mode-v1", "how does batched signing work?");
    template.head_interval = 3;

    let mut room = Room::create(template, room_kp, &alice)?;
    println!("Room:     {}", short(room.world_id().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Creator:  {} (alice)", short(alice.did().as_str(), 32));
    println!("Mode:     head_interval = 3 (sign every 3 appends)");
    println!("Tick = length (a room's time is its record count)");
    println!();

    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    // records.len() = 2, appends = 1, no head yet

    // 10 posts. Heads expected at length 4, 7, 10.
    for i in 1..=10 {
        room.post(
            &bob,
            &format!("message {i}: a small contribution"),
            "normal",
        )?;
        let n = room.heads().len();
        let rec_len = room.records().len();
        if n > 0 {
            let last_head_len = room.heads()[n - 1].length;
            if last_head_len as usize == rec_len {
                println!(
                    "  post {i:>2}: head emitted at length={last_head_len}, last={}...",
                    short(&room.heads()[n - 1].last, 16)
                );
            }
        }
    }
    println!();

    // Summary
    println!("Log length: {}", room.records().len());
    println!("Heads emitted: {}", room.heads().len());
    for (i, head) in room.heads().iter().enumerate() {
        println!(
            "  head {i}: length={}, tick={}, last={}...",
            head.length, head.tick, short(&head.last, 16)
        );
    }
    println!();

    // Uncommitted tail
    let last_head_len = room.heads().last().map(|h| h.length).unwrap_or(0);
    let tail = room.records().len() as u64 - last_head_len;
    if tail > 0 {
        println!(
            "Uncommitted tail: {tail} record(s) beyond the last head (length {last_head_len})."
        );
        println!(
            "  These have author proofs but no head yet; the room has not"
        );
        println!(
            "  committed to them. The next head (at the next interval boundary)"
        );
        println!(
            "  will cover them."
        );
    }
    println!();

    // Verify all heads
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Head verification:");
    for (i, head) in room.heads().iter().enumerate() {
        match room.verify_head(head) {
            Ok(()) => println!("  head {i}: verifies"),
            Err(e) => println!("  head {i}: FAILED: {e}"),
        }
    }
    println!();

    // Room verification
    match verify_room(&room) {
        Ok(()) => println!("Room verifies offline: chain + author proofs + heads."),
        Err(e) => println!("Room FAILED: {e}"),
    }
    println!();

    // Tampering demos
    println!("{}", "-".repeat(74));
    println!("Tampering scenarios:");
    let mut h = room.heads()[0].clone();
    h.last = "00".repeat(32);
    match room.verify_head(&h) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  last tampered: UNEXPECTED pass"),
        Err(_) => println!("  last tampered: correctly fails (chain/last mismatch)"),
    }
    let mut h = room.heads()[0].clone();
    h.tick += 1;
    match room.verify_head(&h) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  tick tampered: UNEXPECTED pass"),
        Err(_) => println!("  tick tampered: correctly fails (tick != length)"),
    }
    let mut h = room.heads()[0].clone();
    h.length += 100;
    match room.verify_head(&h) {
        Ok(()) => println!("  length tampered: UNEXPECTED pass"),
        Err(_) => println!("  length tampered: correctly fails (exceeds log)"),
    }
    println!();

    println!("What this establishes:");
    println!("  - The room's signature moves from each record to the head.");
    println!("    Every record still carries its author's proof; the room");
    println!("    commits to batches via signed heads.");
    println!("  - A head is small: format, world, length, last, tick, proof.");
    println!("    Tick = length: a room's time is its record count.");
    println!("  - A head alone is a binding commitment; a peer can verify");
    println!("    it without the full log. Federation follows.");
    println!("  - Records past the last head are the uncommitted tail;");
    println!("    they have author proofs and join the log's chain, but the");
    println!("    room has not yet committed to them. The next head will.");
    println!("  - The room's signing key need only be live at batch");
    println!("    boundaries. This is the security win.");
    println!();
    println!("Anchor (public domain, Emily Dickinson):");
    println!("  'Forever – is composed of Nows –'");
    println!("  'Tis not a different time –'");

    Ok(())
}
```

---

To check by running: cargo build && cargo test && cargo run --bin chat-demo && cargo run --bin home-demo && cargo run --bin head-demo.

Expected: 30 tests pass (23 from before + 7 new head tests). chat-demo and home-demo behave as before (head_interval=1, per-record). head-demo prints three heads at lengths 4, 7, 10, one uncommitted tail record, and three tampering scenarios that correctly fail.

A note to carry in the reply. Under head mode, Message and Join no longer carry the room's proof. RoomCreated and Refusal do (the seed and the room's own refusals are room-signed regardless). Leave never does. This is the trust-shape change: the room's signature on a Message is now the head that covers it, not a proof on the record itself. The next cycle (succession trio, runtime_attestation, or the plugin protocol) can proceed from here.


::➡️


Step 4 — runtime attestation.

The design in one line. A RuntimeAttestation is a published artifact (like a head — not stored in any log) in which every key that shares a runtime co-signs the same body, declaring the set. Each listed key's proof must be present; every proof must belong to a listed key; the runtime_id must appear in the list. That's it — no state, no new field on Room or Commons, just the record and its verifier.

What it means. Without an attestation, keys are independent (the correct default). With one, a reader knows these keys share a runtime — and still knows each key's KnowledgeWorld is its own chain, because the sandbox is what the key is. The attestation makes the sharing legible; it doesn't create sharing. That's the honesty the standard asks for.

Where it lives. Nowhere in particular — it's published. A room, a Commons, or a peer can hold it (store the JSON in their own records later, if we want that), but the attestation is a fact about a runtime, not an event in a world. Same pattern as WorldHead.

Files touched: src/attestation.rs (new), src/lib.rs (module decl + export), tests/integration.rs (four tests appended). Everything else unchanged.

---

src/attestation.rs (new)

```rust
//! Runtime attestations.
//!
//! A runtime that holds multiple keys may declare them. The
//! attestation is co-signed by every listed key, so no key can be
//! added without its owner's consent. It is a *published artifact*
//! (like a head), not a record in any log. Without one, keys are
//! treated as independent — the correct default. With one, a reader
//! knows the keys share a runtime and *also* knows each key's
//! KnowledgeWorld is its own chain, because isolation is what the
//! key is.

use crate::did::{DidKey, Keypair};
use crate::error::{Error, Result};
use crate::hash;
use crate::proof::{Proof, ProofOrArray};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RuntimeAttestation {
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub type_: String,
    pub runtime_id: DidKey,
    pub keys: Vec<DidKey>,
    pub ts: String,
    pub proof: ProofOrArray,
}

impl RuntimeAttestation {
    pub fn hash(&self) -> Result<String> {
        hash::hash_of(self)
    }
}

/// Sign a runtime attestation. The caller supplies the runtime's
/// key and the full list of keys sharing this runtime. Every
/// listed key must sign; the runtime key is one of them. The
/// runtime_id must appear in the list. Duplicates are refused.
pub fn attest_runtime(
    runtime_kp: &Keypair,
    keys: &[&Keypair],
) -> Result<RuntimeAttestation> {
    let runtime_id = runtime_kp.did();
    let key_ids: Vec<DidKey> = keys.iter().map(|k| k.did()).collect();
    if !key_ids.iter().any(|k| k == &runtime_id) {
        return Err(Error::Policy(
            "runtime_id must appear in the keys list".into(),
        ));
    }
    let mut seen = std::collections::HashSet::new();
    for k in &key_ids {
        if !seen.insert(k.clone()) {
            return Err(Error::Policy(format!(
                "duplicate key: {}",
                k.as_str()
            )));
        }
    }
    let mut att = RuntimeAttestation {
        type_: "runtime_attestation".into(),
        runtime_id,
        keys: key_ids,
        ts: crate::now(),
        proof: ProofOrArray::placeholder(),
    };
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&att)?;
    let mut proofs = Vec::new();
    for kp in keys {
        let sig = kp.sign(&body);
        proofs.push(Proof::create(kp, &sig));
    }
    att.proof = ProofOrArray::array(proofs);
    Ok(att)
}

/// Verify a runtime attestation: structure, every listed key's
/// proof present and valid, no proof for a key not in the list,
/// no duplicates, runtime_id in the list.
pub fn verify_attestation(att: &RuntimeAttestation) -> Result<()> {
    if att.type_ != "runtime_attestation" {
        return Err(Error::Verification("not a runtime_attestation".into()));
    }
    if att.keys.is_empty() {
        return Err(Error::Verification("empty keys list".into()));
    }
    if !att.keys.iter().any(|k| k == &att.runtime_id) {
        return Err(Error::Verification(
            "runtime_id not in keys list".into(),
        ));
    }
    let mut seen = std::collections::HashSet::new();
    for k in &att.keys {
        if !seen.insert(k.clone()) {
            return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                "duplicate key: {}",
                k.as_str()
            )));
        }
    }
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(att)?;
    // Every listed key must have exactly one proof
    for k in &att.keys {
        let p = att.proof.find_by_method(k).ok_or_else(|| {
            Error::Verification(format!("missing proof for {}", k.as_str()))
        })?;
        p.verify(&body)?;
    }
    // Every proof must be for a listed key; count must match
    match &att.proof {
        ProofOrArray::Single(_) => {
            return Err(Error::Verification(
                "attestation must carry one proof per key".into(),
            ));
        }
        ProofOrArray::Array(proofs) => {
            if proofs.len() != att.keys.len() {
                return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                    "proof count {} != key count {}",
                    proofs.len(),
                    att.keys.len()
                )));
            }
            for p in proofs {
                if !att.keys.iter().any(|k| k == &p.verification_method) {
                    return Err(Error::Verification(format!(
                        "proof for {} but not in keys list",
                        p.verification_method.as_str()
                    )));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

src/lib.rs

```rust
pub mod error;
pub mod hash;
pub mod did;
pub mod proof;
pub mod template;
pub mod record;
pub mod room;
pub mod verify;
pub mod transcript;
pub mod home;
pub mod commons;
pub mod head;
pub mod attestation;

pub use did::{DidKey, Keypair};
pub use error::{Error, Result};
pub use head::WorldHead;
pub use proof::{Proof, ProofOrArray, ProofPurpose};
pub use room::{Member, Room};
pub use template::{ModeratorSpec, RoomTemplate};
pub use attestation::{attest_runtime, verify_attestation, RuntimeAttestation};

pub fn now() -> String {
    chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339_opts(chrono::SecondsFormat::Millis, true)
}

pub(crate) fn sign_record<T, F>(
    mut rec: T,
    signer: &Keypair,
    wrap: fn(T) -> record::Record,
    set_proof: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    T: serde::Serialize + Clone,
    F: FnOnce(&mut T, ProofOrArray),
{
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&wrap(rec.clone()))?;
    let sig = signer.sign(&body);
    let proof = ProofOrArray::Single(Proof::create(signer, &sig));
    set_proof(&mut rec, proof);
    Ok(rec)
}

pub(crate) fn sign_record_dual<T, F>(
    mut rec: T,
    subject: &Keypair,
    room: &Keypair,
    wrap: fn(T) -> record::Record,
    set_proof: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    T: serde::Serialize + Clone,
    F: FnOnce(&mut T, ProofOrArray),
{
    let body = hash::body_for_signing(&wrap(rec.clone()))?;
    let subj_sig = subject.sign(&body);
    let room_sig = room.sign(&body);
    let subj_proof = Proof::create(subject, &subj_sig);
    let room_proof = Proof::create(room, &room_sig);
    set_proof(&mut rec, ProofOrArray::array(vec![subj_proof, room_proof]));
    Ok(rec)
}
```

```rust
use chat_world::commons::{verify_commons, Commons};

#[test]
fn restore_makes_text_visible() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    let before = room.visible_log();
    let view = before.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message") && v["redacted"].as_bool() == Some(true))
        .expect("no redacted message in view");
    assert_eq!(view["text"].as_str(), Some("[removed]"));

    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    let after = room.visible_log();
    let view = after.iter()
        .find(|v| v["type"].as_str() == Some("message")
              && v["text"].as_str() == Some("words I take back"))
        .expect("message not visible after restore");
    assert!(view["restored_incidents"].is_array());
    assert_eq!(
        view["restored_incidents"][0]["incident"].as_str(),
        Some(&redact_hash[..16])
    );

    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn redacted_record_unchanged_after_restore() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_room();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    let msg = room.post(&bob, "words I take back", "normal")?;
    let msg_hash = msg.hash()?;
    let redact = room.redact(&bob, &msg_hash, "author_request", "my bad")?;
    let redact_hash = redact.hash()?;

    let dave = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&dave, "control", false)?;
    room.restore_incident(&dave, &redact_hash, "I want it visible")?;

    let stored = room.records().iter()
        .find(|r| r.hash().ok().as_deref() == Some(&msg_hash))
        .expect("original message not found");
    match stored {
        Record::Message(m) => assert_eq!(m.text, "words I take back"),
        _ => panic!("original record was not a message"),
    }

    verify_room(&room)
}

    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn commit_draft_links_home_and_room() -> Result<()> {
    let alice = Keypair::generate();
    let home_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    let bob_room_kp = Keypair::generate();
    let mut home = HomeRoom::create(alice.clone(), home_kp, "test home")?;
    let mut bob_room = Room::create(RoomTemplate::chat("t", "test"), bob_room_kp, &bob)?;
    bob_room.join(&alice, "communicate", false)?;
    let draft = home.draft("hello", Some(bob_room.world_id()))?;
    let draft_hash = draft.hash()?;
    let committed = home.commit_draft_to_room(&draft_hash, &mut bob_room)?;
    match committed {
        Record::Message(m) => {
            assert!(m.text.contains("home_ref="));
            assert!(m.text.contains(&draft_hash[..16]));
        }
        _ => panic!("expected message"),
    }
    verify_room(home.room())?;
    verify_room(&bob_room)?;
    Ok(())
}

// ---- Step 3: signed heads ----

#[test]
fn head_mode_emits_heads_at_interval() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(3);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "one", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "two", "normal")?;
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(room.heads()[0].length, 4);

    room.post(&bob, "three", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "four", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "five", "normal")?;
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(room.heads()[1].length, 7);
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_mode_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "b", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "c", "normal")?;
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn head_mode_author_only_records() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(5);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "hi", "normal")?;
    let mut room_proof_on_message = false;
    let mut room_proof_on_join = false;
    for rec in room.records() {
        let has_room = rec.proof().find_by_method(room.world_id()).is_some();
        match rec {
            Record::Message(_) if has_room => room_proof_on_message = true,
            Record::Join(_) if has_room => room_proof_on_join = true,
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    assert!(!room_proof_on_message, "Message should not have room proof in head mode");
    assert!(!room_proof_on_join, "Join should not have room proof in head mode");
    verify_room(&room)
}

#[test]
fn head_verify_against_prefix() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 1);
    let head = room.heads()[0].clone();
    room.verify_head(&head)?;
    room.post(&bob, "b", "normal")?;
    room.post(&bob, "c", "normal")?;
    assert_eq!(room.heads().len(), 2);
    let head0 = room.heads()[0].clone();
    room.verify_head(&head0)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_rejects_tampered_last() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    let mut head = room.heads()[0].clone();
    head.last = "00".repeat(32);
    assert!(room.verify_head(&head).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn head_rejects_wrong_tick() -> Result<()> {
    let mut room = make_head_mode_room(2);
    let bob = Keypair::generate();
    room.join(&bob, "communicate", false)?;
    room.post(&bob, "a", "normal")?;
    let mut head = room.heads()[0].clone();
    head.tick = head.length + 1;
    assert!(room.verify_head(&head).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn per_record_mode_rejects_heads() -> Result<()> {
    let room = make_room();
    assert!(room.heads().is_empty());
    verify_room(&room)
}

// ---- Step 4: runtime attestation ----

use chat_world::attestation::{attest_runtime, verify_attestation};
use chat_world::ProofOrArray;

#[test]
fn runtime_attestation_verifies() -> Result<()> {
    let runtime = Keypair::generate();
    let a = Keypair::generate();
    let b = Keypair::generate();
    let att = attest_runtime(&runtime, &[&runtime, &a, &b])?;
    verify_attestation(&att)
}

#[test]
fn runtime_attestation_rejects_missing_proof() -> Result<()> {
    let runtime = Keypair::generate();
    let a = Keypair::generate();
    let b = Keypair::generate();
    let att = attest_runtime(&runtime, &[&runtime, &a, &b])?;
    let mut tampered = att.clone();
    if let ProofOrArray::Array(mut proofs) = tampered.proof.clone() {
        proofs.pop();
        tampered.proof = ProofOrArray::Array(proofs);
    }
    assert!(verify_attestation(&tampered).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn runtime_attestation_rejects_extra_proof() -> Result<()> {
    let runtime = Keypair::generate();
    let a = Keypair::generate();
    let att = attest_runtime(&runtime, &[&runtime, &a])?;
    let c = Keypair::generate();
    let body = chat_world::hash::body_for_signing(&att)?;
    let extra_sig = c.sign(&body);
    let extra_proof = chat_world::Proof::create(&c, &extra_sig);
    let mut tampered = att.clone();
    if let ProofOrArray::Array(mut proofs) = tampered.proof.clone() {
        proofs.push(extra_proof);
        tampered.proof = ProofOrArray::Array(proofs);
    }
    assert!(verify_attestation(&tampered).is_err());
    Ok(())
}

#[test]
fn runtime_attestation_rejects_runtime_not_in_keys() -> Result<()> {
    let runtime = Keypair::generate();
    let a = Keypair::generate();
    let r = attest_runtime(&runtime, &[&a]);
    assert!(r.is_err());
    Ok(())
}
```

---

To check by running: cargo build && cargo test. All 34 tests should pass (30 from before + 4 new).

What this establishes. The runtime attestation is the smallest possible piece that makes the sandbox principle legible. Without it, keys are independent — the default, and the right one. With it, a reader sees "these keys share a runtime" and also sees each key's chain is its own. A room, a Commons, or a peer can hold the attestation; the attestation itself is a fact about the runtime, not an event in any world. Same shape as WorldHead — a published artifact with its own verifier.

Next cycle, when you say go: the succession trio (moderator_succession, knowledge_shard_export, sibling_declared). It exercises the head machinery (shard exports use chain_head bindings) and completes the bootstrap→graduate story from the design doc. Three records, three verifier rules, one demo. That will be the largest step since Step 3, and I'll do it as one whole reply.


::Sounds good: ➡️


Step 5 — the succession trio.

Three records: KnowledgeShardExport, ModeratorSuccession, SiblingDeclared. Each verifies independently. Cross-log verification (succession's shard_hash == the export's record hash) is a reader-side check — the crate verifies each log on its own, and the demo shows the cross-check.

Room::declare_succession is triple-signed (outgoing + incoming + room). SiblingDeclared lives in the Commons alongside RoomRegistered, so the Commons's records become an enum. Everything else stays put.
